📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期人乳头瘤病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 在mITT人群中,75名患者中,平均(标准差)年龄为64.0(8.3)岁;4名个体(5%)为女性,71名(95%)为男性;2名(3%)为亚洲人,1名(1%)为黑人,5名(7%)为西班牙裔,71名(95%)为白人。在53名未接受过ICI治疗的参与者中,中央评审者的客观缓解率(ORR)为34.0%,中位无进展生存期(PFS)为5.3个月(95%置信区间,2.10-9.00),中位总生存期(OS)为39.3个月(95%置信区间,23.90至不可评估)。在22名对ICI耐药的患者中,未观察到客观反应,且主要终点(中央评审者的ORR)未达成,但中位总生存期(OS)为14.8个月(95%置信区间,8.50-26.00)。
  • circE7 中 m6A 基序的 突变 被发现对其形成至关重要,并且还导致 E6*I 同工型的线性剪接增加。此外,circE7中IRES样基序的突变显着降低了E7蛋白的表达,而YTHDC1的敲低则降低了circE7 RNA和蛋白水平。
  • 宫颈病变HPV阳性,P16表达,而卵巢肿瘤HPV阴性,P16阴性,P53阳性。患者经过手术和四个周期的卡铂/紫杉醇辅助化疗后获得完全缓解,24个月随访时未出现复发。 [10.1186/s12905-026-04834-z]。
  • PCR-LbCas12a 检测显示灵敏度为 91.3% (95% CI 71.9-98.9),特异性为 100%,HPV16 的 LOD 为 100 个拷贝,HPV18 的 LOD 为 10 个拷贝。它可以检测含有不同浓度每种基因型的混合样本中的 HPV16 和 HPV18。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Study retention was excellent (380/389, 97.7%).
  • Of the 75 patients in the mITT population, the mean (SD) age was 64.0 (8.3) years; 4 individuals (5%) were female and 71 (95%) were male; and 2 individuals (3%) were Asian, 1 (1%) was Black, 5 (7%) were Hispanic, and 71 (95%) were White.
  • Mutation of the m6A motif in circE7 was found to be essential for its formation, and also led to increased linear splicing of the E6*I isoform.
  • The cervical lesion was HPV positive with P16 expression, while the ovarian tumor demonstrated HPV negativity with P16 negativity and P53 positivity.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: JMIR Public Health and Surveillance  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-16  |  DOI: 10.2196/104529  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 12, e104529
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

在沙特阿拉伯女性中,文化定制的教育干预对人乳头瘤病毒知识和疫苗接种意愿的影响:准实验研究。

Impact of a Culturally Tailored Educational Intervention on Human Papillomavirus Knowledge and Vaccination Intentions Among Women in Saudi Arabia: Quasi-Experimental Study.
作者： Ebtesam A. Elsayed, Fathia Ahmed Mersal, Heba Ahmed Osman Mohamed, Ala M. Aljehani, Najla M. Aljehani
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒,HPV)是全球最常见的性传播感染之一。尽管有有效的疫苗(抗病毒药物)可用,但在许多地区,包括沙特阿拉伯,其接种率仍不理想,这与文化敏感性、有限的认知以及疫苗(抗病毒药物)接种犹豫有关。
设计与对象
在沙特阿拉伯北方边境地区初级卫生保健中心参加活动的女性中,采用准实验设计(基线前测-后测与对照组)。共有380名参与者,其中190名在对照组,190名在干预组,分别在基线(T1)、干预后1周(T2)和随访3个月(T3)时完成调查,评估与HPV相关的知识、健康意识、态度和疫苗接种(抗病毒药物接种)意愿。
核心方法
教育干预包括一项文化适应性、基于证据的、45分钟的互动小组课程,由初级卫生保健中心提供。
主要结果
Study retention was excellent (380/389, 97.7%). At the 3-month follow-up, intervention participants demonstrated medium to large effect size improvements compared with controls: knowledge (adjusted β=0.84, 95% CI 0.53-1.15; P .10), suggesting consistent intervention effects across demographic characteristics.
解释与创新
这项文化定制的教育干预在提高沙特阿拉伯北方女性对HPV相关知识、态度、健康意识和疫苗接种(抗病毒药物接种)意愿方面是有效的。基于行为理论的教育干预,如信息-动机-行为技能(IMB)模型,有助于解决HPV 疫苗接种(抗病毒药物接种)的信息和动机障碍。
科研与公卫意义
鉴于在疫苗接种(抗病毒药物接种)接种中已记录的意愿与行为之间的差距,未来的研究应纳入已验证的疫苗(抗病毒药物)接种启动和完成结果,并进行更长时间的随访,以评估增强的意愿是否转化为实际的疫苗(抗病毒药物)接种行为。本研究提供了证据,表明文化定制的教育干预可以提高沙特阿拉伯北方女性对HPV相关知识和疫苗接种(抗病毒药物接种)意愿的理解。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究的局限性。由于采用准实验设计,并且主要基于自我报告的结果,而未验证实际的疫苗(抗病毒药物)接种启动或完成情况,因此该研究的证据强度中等。

Impact of a Culturally Tailored Educational Intervention on Human Papillomavirus Knowledge and Vaccination Intentions Among Women in Saudi Arabia: Quasi-Experimental Study.

Authors: Ebtesam A. Elsayed, Fathia Ahmed Mersal, Heba Ahmed Osman Mohamed, Ala M. Aljehani, Najla M. Aljehani
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Human papillomavirus (HPV) is one of the most common sexually transmitted infections worldwide. Despite the availability of effective vaccines, uptake remains suboptimal in many settings, including Saudi Arabia, where cultural sensitivities, limited awareness, and vaccine hesitancy have been documented. Educational interventions grounded in behavioral theory, such as the information-motivation-behavioral skills (IMB) model, can help address informational and motivational barriers to HPV vaccination. OBJECTIVE: This study aimed to evaluate the impact of an educational intervention in improving HPV vaccination decision-making among women in Northern Saudi Arabia. METHODS: A quasi-experimental design (pretest-posttest with a control group) was used among women attending primary health care centers in the Northern Border Region of Saudi Arabia. A total of 380 participants, 190 in the control group and 190 in the intervention group, completed surveys at baseline (T1), 1-week post intervention (T2), and a 3-month follow-up (T3), assessing HPV-related knowledge, health awareness, attitudes, and vaccination intentions. The educational intervention consisted of a culturally adapted, evidence-based, 45-minute interactive group session delivered in primary health care centers. RESULTS: Study retention was excellent (380/389, 97.7%). At the 3-month follow-up, intervention participants demonstrated medium to large effect size improvements compared with controls: knowledge (adjusted β=0.84, 95% CI 0.53-1.15; P .10), suggesting consistent intervention effects across demographic characteristics. CONCLUSIONS: This culturally tailored educational intervention was effective in improving HPV-related knowledge, attitudes, health awareness, and vaccination intentions among women in Northern Saudi Arabia. Given the well-documented intention-behavior gap in vaccination uptake, future research should incorporate verified vaccine initiation and completion outcomes, along with longer follow-up, to assess whether enhanced intentions translate into actual vaccine uptake.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is one of the most common sexually transmitted infections worldwide. Despite the availability of effective vaccines, uptake remains suboptimal in many settings, including Saudi Arabia, where cultural sensitivities, limited awareness, and vaccine hesitancy have been documented.
Design and population
A quasi-experimental design (pretest-posttest with a control group) was used among women attending primary health care centers in the Northern Border Region of Saudi Arabia. A total of 380 participants, 190 in the control group and 190 in the intervention group, completed surveys at baseline (T1), 1-week post intervention (T2), and a 3-month follow-up (T3), assessing HPV-related knowledge, health awareness, attitudes, and vaccination intentions.
Core methods
The educational intervention consisted of a culturally adapted, evidence-based, 45-minute interactive group session delivered in primary health care centers.
Main results
Study retention was excellent (380/389, 97.7%). At the 3-month follow-up, intervention participants demonstrated medium to large effect size improvements compared with controls: knowledge (adjusted β=0.84, 95% CI 0.53-1.15; P .10), suggesting consistent intervention effects across demographic characteristics.
Interpretation and novelty
This culturally tailored educational intervention was effective in improving HPV-related knowledge, attitudes, health awareness, and vaccination intentions among women in Northern Saudi Arabia. Educational interventions grounded in behavioral theory, such as the information-motivation-behavioral skills (IMB) model, can help address informational and motivational barriers to HPV vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
Given the well-documented intention-behavior gap in vaccination uptake, future research should incorporate verified vaccine initiation and completion outcomes, along with longer follow-up, to assess whether enhanced intentions translate into actual vaccine uptake. This study provides evidence that culturally tailored educational interventions can improve HPV-related knowledge and vaccination intentions among women in Northern Saudi Arabia.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The study's evidence is moderate in strength due to the quasi-experimental design and the focus on self-reported outcomes without verification of actual vaccine initiation or completion.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

PDS0101 与 Pembrolizumab 联合治疗 HPV16 阳性复发/转移性头颈鳞状细胞癌:2 期非随机临床试验。

PDS0101 With Pembrolizumab in HPV16-Positive Recurrent/Metastatic Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Phase 2 Nonrandomized Clinical Trial.
作者： Jared Weiss, John Kaczmar, Ashish Chintakuntlawar, Francis P. Worden, Ranee Mehra, Hyunseok Kang, Ralph G. Zinner, Susanne M. Arnold, Priyanka Bhateja, Kevin Harrington
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
人乳头瘤病毒 16 型(HPV16 阳性)复发/转移性 (R/M) 头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 的 发病率 正在上升,批准的疗法可为少数患者提供持久的缓解。
设计与对象
VERSATILE-002是一项2期、开放标签、多中心非随机临床试验,于2021年3月29日至2025年5月15日在美国、英国和爱尔兰的26个肿瘤中心使用西蒙2阶段优化设计进行。88 名经组织学证实为 R/M HPV16 阳性 HNSCC 的参与者入组,其中 87 名(98.9%)接受了 治疗 治疗,构成安全人群。75 例 (85.2%) 属于用于疗效分析的 mITT 人群,其中 53 例 (70.7%) 未接受过 ICI,程序性细胞死亡配体 1 综合阳性评分为 1 或更高,22 例 (29.3%) 具有 ICI 耐药性。
核心方法
PDS0101 是一种治疗性 HPV16 靶向免疫疗法,与派姆单抗(200 毫克,静脉注射,每 3 周一次,最多 35 个周期)加皮下注射 PDS0101(每个方案 5 次预定剂量)相结合。对 mITT 人群进行了功效分析,安全性分析包括所有接受 1 剂或以上剂量的参与者。
主要结果
在mITT人群中,75名患者中,平均(标准差)年龄为64.0(8.3)岁;4名个体(5%)为女性,71名(95%)为男性;2名(3%)为亚洲人,1名(1%)为黑人,5名(7%)为西班牙裔,71名(95%)为白人。在53名未接受过ICI治疗的参与者中,中央评审者的客观缓解率(ORR)为34.0%,中位无进展生存期(PFS)为5.3个月(95%置信区间,2.10-9.00),中位总生存期(OS)为39.3个月(95%置信区间,23.90至不可评估)。在22名对ICI耐药的患者中,未观察到客观反应,且主要终点(中央评审者的ORR)未达成,但中位总生存期(OS)为14.8个月(95%置信区间,8.50-26.00)。
解释与创新
治疗 的耐受性良好,13.8% 的患者经历了 3 级或更高级别的 治疗 相关不良事件。结果表明,PDS0101 联合派姆单抗在 ICI 初治 HPV16 阳性 R/M HNSCC 中显示出抗肿瘤活性,具有良好的安全性、缓解率和生存结果,支持在一线环境中进行进一步评估。
科研与公卫意义
这些研究结果支持在一线环境中进一步评估 PDS0101 联合派姆单抗治疗 ICI 初治 HPV16 阳性 R/M HNSCC,有可能改善结果和安全性。
局限与证据强度
该研究是非随机的,这限制了得出因果结论的能力。样本量相对较小,并且该研究不包括对照组,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。证据强度中等。

PDS0101 With Pembrolizumab in HPV16-Positive Recurrent/Metastatic Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma: A Phase 2 Nonrandomized Clinical Trial.

Authors: Jared Weiss, John Kaczmar, Ashish Chintakuntlawar, Francis P. Worden, Ranee Mehra, Hyunseok Kang, Ralph G. Zinner, Susanne M. Arnold, Priyanka Bhateja, Kevin Harrington
Original abstract (English)
IMPORTANCE: The incidence of human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16-positive) recurrent/metastatic (R/M) head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) is rising. Approved therapies offer a durable response to a minority of patients. OBJECTIVE: To determine the efficacy and safety of a therapeutic HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, PDS0101, in combination with pembrolizumab in participants with HPV16-positive R/M HNSCC, with separate evaluation of immune checkpoint inhibitor (ICI)-naive and ICI-resistant cohorts. DESIGN, SETTING, AND PARTICIPANTS: VERSATILE-002 was a phase 2, open-label, multicenter nonrandomized clinical trial conducted at 26 oncology centers from March 29, 2021, to May 15, 2025, across the US, UK, and Ireland using the Simon 2-stage optimal design. Efficacy analyses were performed on modified intent-to-treat (mITT) population; safety analyses included all participants who received 1 or more doses. Eighty-eight participants with histologically confirmed R/M HPV16-positive HNSCC were enrolled, of whom 87 (98.9%) received treatment and composed the safety population. Seventy five (85.2%) were in the mITT population used for efficacy analyses, of whom 53 (70.7%) were ICI-naive with a programmed cell death ligand 1 combined positive score of 1 or greater and 22 (29.3%) were ICI-resistant. EXPOSURES: Pembrolizumab (200 mg, intravenously, every 3 weeks for up to 35 cycles) plus subcutaneous PDS0101 (5 scheduled doses per protocol). MAIN OUTCOMES AND MEASURES: The primary end point was objective response rate (ORR) per the Response Evaluation Criteria in Solid Tumors, version 1.1, by a masked independent central reviewer. Secondary end points included progression-free survival (PFS), median overall survival (mOS), and safety, with exploratory end points of duration of response, disease control rate, and response rates by programmed cell death ligand 1 status. RESULTS: Of the 75 patients in the mITT population, the mean (SD) age was 64.0 (8.3) years; 4 individuals (5%) were female and 71 (95%) were male; and 2 individuals (3%) were Asian, 1 (1%) was Black, 5 (7%) were Hispanic, and 71 (95%) were White. Among 53 ICI-naive participants in the mITT cohort, ORR by the central reviewer was 34.0%, median PFS was 5.3 months (95% CI, 2.10-9.00), and mOS was 39.3 months (95% CI, 23.90 to not evaluable). Among 22 ICI-resistant patients in the mITT cohort, no objective responses were observed and the primary end point (ORR by central reviewer) was not met, but mOS was 14.8 months (95% CI, 8.50-26.00). The treatment was well tolerated, with 13.8% experiencing grade 3 or higher treatment-related adverse events. CONCLUSIONS AND RELEVANCE: The results of this nonrandomized clinical trial suggest that PDS0101 plus pembrolizumab showed antitumor activity in ICI-naive HPV16-positive R/M HNSCC, with favorable safety, response rates, and survival outcomes. The findings support further evaluation in a first-line setting. TRIAL REGISTRATION: ClinicalTrials.gov Identifier: NCT04260126.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The incidence of human papillomavirus type 16 (HPV16-positive) recurrent/metastatic (R/M) head and neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) is rising, and approved therapies offer a durable response to a minority of patients.
Design and population
VERSATILE-002 was a phase 2, open-label, multicenter nonrandomized clinical trial conducted at 26 oncology centers from March 29, 2021, to May 15, 2025, across the US, UK, and Ireland using the Simon 2-stage optimal design. Eighty-eight participants with histologically confirmed R/M HPV16-positive HNSCC were enrolled, of whom 87 (98.9%) received treatment and composed the safety population. Seventy five (85.2%) were in the mITT population used for efficacy analyses, of whom 53 (70.7%) were ICI-naive with a programmed cell death ligand 1 combined positive score of 1 or greater and 22 (29.3%) were ICI-resistant.
Core methods
PDS0101, a therapeutic HPV16-targeted immunotherapy, was combined with pembrolizumab (200 mg, intravenously, every 3 weeks for up to 35 cycles) plus subcutaneous PDS0101 (5 scheduled doses per protocol). Efficacy analyses were performed on the mITT population, and safety analyses included all participants who received 1 or more doses.
Main results
Of the 75 patients in the mITT population, the mean (SD) age was 64.0 (8.3) years; 4 individuals (5%) were female and 71 (95%) were male; and 2 individuals (3%) were Asian, 1 (1%) was Black, 5 (7%) were Hispanic, and 71 (95%) were White. Among 53 ICI-naive participants, the ORR by the central reviewer was 34.0%, median PFS was 5.3 months (95% CI, 2.10-9.00), and mOS was 39.3 months (95% CI, 23.90 to not evaluable). Among 22 ICI-resistant patients, no objective responses were observed, and the primary end point (ORR by central reviewer) was not met, but mOS was 14.8 months (95% CI, 8.50-26.00).
Interpretation and novelty
The treatment was well tolerated, with 13.8% experiencing grade 3 or higher treatment-related adverse events. The results suggest that PDS0101 plus pembrolizumab showed antitumor activity in ICI-naive HPV16-positive R/M HNSCC, with favorable safety, response rates, and survival outcomes, supporting further evaluation in a first-line setting.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support further evaluation of PDS0101 plus pembrolizumab in a first-line setting for ICI-naive HPV16-positive R/M HNSCC, with potential for improved outcomes and safety.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was nonrandomized, which limits the ability to draw causal conclusions. The sample size was relatively small, and the study did not include a control group, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

HPV16 CircE7 RNA 的调节和功能。

Regulation and function of the HPV16 CircE7 RNA.
作者： Eunice E. Lee, Yihui Huang, Eleanor Dowell, Kaitlyn Walsh, Calvin Hosler, Austin Witt, Odera Ifeacho, Doreen Palsgrove, Andrew Day, Claire James
摘要中文翻译摘要 高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),包括 HPV16,产生包含 E7 癌基因 (circE7) 的环状 RNA。CircE7 可在 HPV16 阳性细胞和肿瘤中检测到,优先定位于细胞质,经过 N 6 -甲基腺苷 (m 6 A) 修饰,可翻译为 E7 癌蛋白。在这里,我们探讨了circE7的调控和功能。后剪接点两侧的 m 6 A 基序的 突变 揭示了单个 m 6 A 基序对于 circE7 的形成至关重要。该 m 6 A 基序的 突变 促进了 E6*I 剪接位点 (226^409) 的线性剪接,表明线性和环状 E7 剪接是反向调节的。此外,circE7 中 IRES 样基序的 突变 显着降低了 E7 蛋白表达,但对 circE7 RNA 水平没有显着影响。YTHDC1 的敲低(而非其他 m 6 A 结合蛋白)降低了 circE7 RNA 和蛋白质的表达。BaseScope ISH用于确认头颈鳞状细胞癌细胞系和肿瘤中circE7的表达。使用 qRT-PCR 和 BaseScope ISH,我们发现血清和氨基酸饥饿显着增加了 circE7。最后,我们在 circE7 m 6 A 基序 (Mut2) 中生成了带有 突变 的 HPV16 基因组。用 Mut2 对原代角质形成细胞进行稳定转导,证实了 circE7 的缺失和 E6*I 表达的增加。Mut2 HPV16 基因组 表现出病毒复制显着降低,但转化原代角质形成细胞的能力增强。我们的研究表明,m 6 A 对 circE7 和 E6*I 的精确调控对于 HPV16 感染和转化角质形成细胞的能力至关重要。重要性 高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV),例如 HPV16,必须仔细控制其产生的 E6 和 E7 蛋白数量。这项研究表明,HPV16 会切换病毒 RNA 上的单个化学标签(m6A 标记)来控制早期区域 RNA 的产生,包括称为 circE7 的环状 RNA。同一位点协调调节 E6*I 同工型的剪接。CircE7 使用 m6A 结合蛋白来控制其产生,并使用特定序列来促进其翻译。它存在于 HPV 阳性癌症中,可以对营养缺乏做出反应。通过该 m 6 A 位点对 circE7 的调节也影响病毒复制和转化能力,表明这种调节机制对于 HPV 生物学至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在研究 HPV16 产生的 circE7 RNA 的调控和功能,因为 高危型HPV(包括 HPV16)必须仔细控制 E6 和 E7 蛋白的产生以维持其致癌潜力。
设计与对象
该研究使用 HPV16 阳性细胞系和原代角质形成细胞进行,实验涉及 m6A 基序和 IRES 样序列的 突变,以及 YTHDC1 等 m6A 结合蛋白的敲低。
核心方法
研究人员使用qRT-PCR和BaseScope ISH检测circE7 RNA,并生成了circE7 m6A基序中带有突变的HPV16 基因组,以评估其对病毒复制和转化的影响。
主要结果
circE7 中 m6A 基序的 突变 被发现对其形成至关重要,并且还导致 E6*I 同工型的线性剪接增加。此外,circE7中IRES样基序的突变显着降低了E7蛋白的表达,而YTHDC1的敲低则降低了circE7 RNA和蛋白水平。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,m6A 对 circE7 和 E6*I 的调节是 HPV16 控制其感染和角质形成细胞转化的关键机制,凸显了 HPV 生物学中的一条新的调节途径。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 HPV16 发病机制 分子机制的深入了解,这可以为 HPV 相关癌症的靶向治疗或诊断工具的开发提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体外和细胞系模型,证据基于实验操作而不是临床结果,这表明对研究结果的可信度中等。

Regulation and function of the HPV16 CircE7 RNA.

Authors: Eunice E. Lee, Yihui Huang, Eleanor Dowell, Kaitlyn Walsh, Calvin Hosler, Austin Witt, Odera Ifeacho, Doreen Palsgrove, Andrew Day, Claire James
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT High-risk human papillomaviruses (HPV), including HPV16, produce circular RNA that encompasses the E7 oncogene (circE7). CircE7 can be detected in HPV16-positive cells and tumors, is preferentially localized to the cytoplasm, is N 6 -methyladenosine (m 6 A)-modified, and can be translated into the E7 oncoprotein. Here, we explored the regulation and function of circE7. Mutation of m 6 A motifs flanking the backsplice junction revealed a single m 6 A motif to be essential for circE7 formation. Mutation of this m 6 A motif promoted linear splicing of the E6*I splice site (226^409), suggesting that linear and circular E7 splicing are inversely regulated. Additionally, mutation of an IRES-like motif in circE7 significantly decreased E7 protein expression, without having significant effects on circE7 RNA levels. Knockdown of YTHDC1, but not other m 6 A-binding proteins, decreased both circE7 RNA and protein expression. BaseScope ISH was used to confirm the expression of circE7 in head and neck squamous cell carcinoma cell lines and tumors. Using both qRT-PCR and BaseScope ISH, we found that serum and amino acid starvation significantly increased circE7. Finally, we generated an HPV16 genome with mutations in the circE7 m 6 A motif (Mut2). Stable transduction of primary keratinocytes with Mut2 confirmed the loss of circE7 and increased expression of E6*I. The Mut2 HPV16 genome exhibited significantly decreased viral replication but an increased ability to transform primary keratinocytes. Our studies reveal that the precise regulation of circE7 and E6*I by m 6 A is critical for the ability of HPV16 to infect and transform keratinocytes. IMPORTANCE High-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs), such as HPV16, must carefully control how much E6 and E7 proteins they make. This study shows that HPV16 toggles a single chemical tag on the viral RNA (an m6A mark) to control the production of early region RNAs, including a circular RNA called circE7. The same site coordinately regulates splicing of the E6*I isoform. CircE7 uses m6A-binding proteins to control its production and a specific sequence to promote its translation. It is present in HPV-positive cancers and can respond to nutrient starvation. Regulation of circE7 through this m 6 A site also impacted viral replication and transformation capacity, indicating that this regulatory mechanism is critical for HPV biology.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the regulation and function of circE7 RNA produced by HPV16, as high-risk HPVs, including HPV16, must carefully control E6 and E7 protein production to maintain their oncogenic potential.
Design and population
The study was conducted using HPV16-positive cell lines and primary keratinocytes, with experiments involving mutation of m6A motifs and IRES-like sequences, as well as knockdown of m6A-binding proteins like YTHDC1.
Core methods
The researchers used qRT-PCR and BaseScope ISH to detect circE7 RNA, and generated an HPV16 genome with mutations in the circE7 m6A motif to assess its effects on viral replication and transformation.
Main results
Mutation of the m6A motif in circE7 was found to be essential for its formation, and also led to increased linear splicing of the E6*I isoform. Additionally, mutation of an IRES-like motif in circE7 significantly decreased E7 protein expression, while knockdown of YTHDC1 reduced both circE7 RNA and protein levels.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that the regulation of circE7 and E6*I by m6A is a critical mechanism for HPV16 to control its infection and transformation of keratinocytes, highlighting a novel regulatory pathway in HPV biology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the molecular mechanisms of HPV16 pathogenesis, which could inform the development of targeted therapies or diagnostic tools for HPV-associated cancers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro and cell line models, and the evidence is based on experimental manipulation rather than clinical outcomes, which suggests a moderate confidence in the findings.

PCR-LbCas12a 检测用于 HPV 16 和 18 的基因分型。

PCR-LbCas12a Assay for Genotyping of HPV 16 and 18.
作者： ChonIl Paek, Jin Zhou, Hui-Zheng Sin
摘要中文翻译CRISPR/Cas12a系统因其高特异性和反式切割活性而被广泛应用于核酸检测。我们将CRISPR/Cas12a系统的特异性与PCR的敏感性相结合,开发出一种具有高特异性和敏感性的新型HPV检测方法,称为PCR-LbCas12a检测方法。在这里,我们用共有引物 (MY09/11) 预扩增了几乎所有 高危型HPV 基因型,并添加到含有 HPV 16 或 18 CRISPR/Cas12a 系统的新管中进行 HPV 基因分型。PCR-LbCas12a 对 HPV16 或 HPV18 DNA 的 LOD 分别为每个反应 100 个拷贝和 10 个拷贝。在HPV 16和HPV 18 DNA的混合样本中,PCR-LbCas12a检测可以从含有10 2 个HPV16拷贝和10 4 个HPV18拷贝的样本中检测到HPV16 DNA。另一方面,从含有10 3 个HPV16和HPV18拷贝的样本中分别检测到HPV18 DNA。在使用 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性细胞系(SiHa、HeLa 和 C-33A)进行验证后,使用先前通过类型特异性 qPCR 表征的 39 个临床样本对 PCR-LbCas12a 检测进行了评估。该检测的灵敏度为 91.3 (95 CI 71.9-98.9),特异性为 100。PCR-LbCas12a 检测可以区分 HPV 16 和 HPV 18 基因型,在同时检测包括 HPV 16 和 HPV18 在内的大多数 高危型HPV 基因型方面具有巨大潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在开发一种具有高特异性和敏感性的新型 HPV 检测方法,称为 PCR-LbCas12a 检测方法,以满足改进 HPV 基因分型方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性细胞系以及 39 个临床样本开发和验证 PCR-LbCas12a 检测方法。
核心方法
该检测结合了 CRISPR/Cas12a 系统的特异性和 PCR 的敏感性,使用共有引物 (MY09/11) 进行预扩增,使用 HPV 16 或 18 CRISPR/Cas12a 系统进行基因分型。
主要结果
PCR-LbCas12a 检测显示灵敏度为 91.3% (95% CI 71.9-98.9),特异性为 100%,HPV16 的 LOD 为 100 个拷贝,HPV18 的 LOD 为 10 个拷贝。它可以检测含有不同浓度每种基因型的混合样本中的 HPV16 和 HPV18。
解释与创新
作者将结果解释为证明了 PCR-LbCas12a 检测同时检测大多数 高危型HPV 基因型(包括 HPV16 和 HPV18)的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,PCR-LbCas12a 检测可用于临床环境中同时检测 高危型HPV 基因型,从而有可能提高诊断准确性和患者管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用有限数量的临床样本以及需要在更大人群中进一步验证。由于样本量小且缺乏普遍性,证据强度中等。

PCR-LbCas12a Assay for Genotyping of HPV 16 and 18.

Authors: ChonIl Paek, Jin Zhou, Hui-Zheng Sin
Original abstract (English)
CRISPR/Cas12a systems have been widely used in nucleic acid detection, because of their high specificity and trans-cleavage activity. We combined the specificity of CRISPR/Cas12a system with the sensitivity of PCR, to develop a new HPV detection assay with high specificity and sensitivity, called PCR-LbCas12a assay. Here, we pre-amplified almost all high-risk HPV genotypes with consensus primer (MY09/11) and added to new tubes containing HPV 16 or 18 CRISPR/Cas12a system for HPV genotyping. The LOD of PCR-LbCas12a for HPV16 or HPV18 DNA was 100 copies and 10 copies per reaction, respectively. In mixed samples of HPV 16 and HPV 18 DNA, PCR-LbCas12a assay could detect HPV16 DNA from samples containing 10 2 copies of HPV16 and 10 4 copies of HPV18. On the other hand, HPV18 DNA was detected from samples containing 10 3 copies of HPV16 and HPV18, respectively. Following validation with HPV-positive and HPV-negative cell lines (SiHa, HeLa, and C-33A), the PCR-LbCas12a assay was evaluated using 39 clinical samples previously characterized by type-specific qPCR. The assay demonstrated a sensitivity of 91.3 (95 CI 71.9-98.9) and a specificity of 100. The PCR-LbCas12a assay can discriminate the HPV 16 and HPV 18 genotypes, and holds great potential in simultaneous detection of most high-risk HPV genotypes including HPV 16 and HPV18.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to develop a new HPV detection assay with high specificity and sensitivity, called PCR-LbCas12a assay, to address the need for improved methods in HPV genotyping.
Design and population
The study involved the development and validation of the PCR-LbCas12a assay using HPV-positive and HPV-negative cell lines and 39 clinical samples.
Core methods
The assay combined the specificity of CRISPR/Cas12a system with the sensitivity of PCR, using consensus primer (MY09/11) for pre-amplification and HPV 16 or 18 CRISPR/Cas12a system for genotyping.
Main results
The PCR-LbCas12a assay demonstrated a sensitivity of 91.3% (95% CI 71.9-98.9) and a specificity of 100%, with a LOD of 100 copies for HPV16 and 10 copies for HPV18. It could detect HPV16 and HPV18 in mixed samples containing varying concentrations of each genotype.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the results as demonstrating the potential of the PCR-LbCas12a assay for simultaneous detection of most high-risk HPV genotypes, including HPV16 and HPV18.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that the PCR-LbCas12a assay could be useful in clinical settings for the simultaneous detection of high-risk HPV genotypes, potentially improving diagnostic accuracy and patient management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of a limited number of clinical samples and the need for further validation in larger populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and lack of generalizability.

从孟加拉国头颈鳞状细胞癌组织中分离出的 HPV 16 和 HPV 18 中 L1 基因的部分序列。

Partial sequence of L1 gene in HPV 16 and HPV 18 isolated from head-neck squamous cell carcinoma tissues in Bangladesh.
作者： Islam SMRU, Md. Sayeam Khandaker, Bishnu Pada Dey, Syed Farhan Ali Razib, Kabir Hossen, Joy Prokash Debnath, Shamim Ahmed, Afzalun Nessa, S. M. Rashed Ul Islam
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 是头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 进展的最关键决定因素之一。在这里,我们报告了两种高危病原体 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 的部分 L1 基因序列,这些序列是通过从孟加拉国 HNSCC 患者的多个头颈组织区域进行活检提取的。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查孟加拉国头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 组织中高危 人乳头瘤病毒(HPV-16 和 HPV-18)的部分 L1 基因序列,以满足更好地了解该地区 HPV 相关癌症的需要。

研究人员利用活检从 HNSCC 组织中提取了 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 的部分 L1 基因序列。
主要结果
该研究报告了孟加拉国 HNSCC 组织中 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 的部分 L1 基因序列。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为有助于了解孟加拉国 HPV 相关的 HNSCC,强调了这些 高危型HPV 类型在此背景下的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测 和 诊断 提供孟加拉国 HPV 相关 HNSCC 的信息,可能有助于制定有针对性的干预措施和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量小以及具体关注两种 HPV 类型,这可能会限制普遍性。证据强度中等。

Partial sequence of L1 gene in HPV 16 and HPV 18 isolated from head-neck squamous cell carcinoma tissues in Bangladesh.

Authors: Islam SMRU, Md. Sayeam Khandaker, Bishnu Pada Dey, Syed Farhan Ali Razib, Kabir Hossen, Joy Prokash Debnath, Shamim Ahmed, Afzalun Nessa, S. M. Rashed Ul Islam
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Human papillomavirus is one of the most crucial determinants responsible for the progression of head-neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC). Here, we report partial L1 gene sequences of two high-risk etiological agents, HPV-16 and HPV-18, extracted through biopsies from multiple head-neck tissue regions of HNSCC patients in Bangladesh.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the partial L1 gene sequences of high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPV-16 and HPV-18) in head-neck squamous cell carcinoma (HNSCC) tissues in Bangladesh, addressing the need for better understanding of HPV-associated cancers in this region.
Design and population
The study involved the extraction of biopsies from multiple head-neck tissue regions of HNSCC patients in Bangladesh, focusing on two high-risk HPV types, HPV-16 and HPV-18.
Core methods
The researchers extracted partial L1 gene sequences of HPV-16 and HPV-18 from HNSCC tissues using biopsies.
Main results
The study reported partial L1 gene sequences of HPV-16 and HPV-18 from HNSCC tissues in Bangladesh.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as contributing to the understanding of HPV-associated HNSCC in Bangladesh, highlighting the importance of these high-risk HPV types in this context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and diagnosis of HPV-associated HNSCC in Bangladesh, potentially aiding in the development of targeted interventions and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the small sample size and the specific focus on two HPV types, which may limit generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.

在 HIV 感染女性中减少非价 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 给药方案的临床试验的基本原理和设计:NOVA-HIV 研究。

Rationale and design of a clinical trial of a reduced dosing schedule for the nonavalent human papillomavirus vaccine in women living with HIV: The NOVA-HIV study.
作者： Emma Karlsen, Jenna Langlois, Isabelle Boucoiran, Jason Brophy, Chelsea Elwood, Catherine Hankins, Alicia Hornsberger, Mel Krajden, Marette Lee, Mona Loutfy
摘要中文翻译几乎所有 宫颈癌 病例都是由 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 引起。女性和宫颈感染艾滋病毒的人(女性艾滋病毒感染者)受到 宫颈癌 的影响尤为严重;与未感染 HIV 的女性相比,她们的 HPV 感染率更高,并且对持续 HPV 感染的易感性更高。HPV 疫苗接种 对于预防 宫颈癌 非常有效,世界卫生组织目前建议免疫功能正常的女性注射一剂或两剂 HPV 疫苗接种 方案。尽管已批准的非价 HPV (9vHPV) 疫苗 对于未感染 HIV 的患者具有高度安全性、免疫原性和有效性,但针对感染 HIV 的女性的数据却很少。为了弥补这一知识差距,NOVA-HIV 研究被设计为一项针对 18-45 岁 HIV 感染女性的 9vHPV 疫苗 加拿大多中心、观察者盲法、随机 IV 期临床试验。主要目的是确定两剂 9vHPV 疫苗 后的峰值抗 HPV16/18 几何平均滴度 (GMT) 是否不劣于三剂后的峰值抗 HPV16/18 GMT。该试验将招募来自加拿大五个省份 11 个地点的 275 名感染艾滋病毒的女性。参与者以 1:1 的比例随机分配,按常规计划(0/2/6 个月)或延长计划(0/6/12 个月)接受 9vHPV 疫苗。在 24 个月内,参与者会进行六到七次就诊,收集临床数据、血液和阴道样本。生成的数据可以支持在感染艾滋病毒的女性中实施减少剂量的方案,使 疫苗接种 更加可行,有助于消除 宫颈癌,并加速全球健康公平。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究需要解决感染艾滋病毒的女性中 HPV 感染率高以及对持续 HPV 感染的易感性增加的问题,因为这些人受到 宫颈癌 的影响尤为严重。该研究旨在确定 9vHPV 疫苗 的减量剂量方案是否不劣于 HIV 女性感染者的标准方案。
设计与对象
NOVA-HIV 研究是一项加拿大多中心、观察者盲法、随机、IV 期临床试验,涉及 275 名年龄在 18-45 岁的 HIV 女性,按 1:1 随机分配,按常规方案(0/2/6 个月)或延长方案(0/6/12 个月)接受 9vHPV 疫苗。
核心方法
参与者被随机分配接受常规时间表(0/2/6 个月)或延长时间表(0/6/12 个月)的 9vHPV 疫苗。在 24 个月内,参与者参加六到七次访问,收集临床数据、血液和阴道样本。
主要结果
该研究旨在确定两剂 9vHPV 疫苗 后的峰值抗 HPV16/18 几何平均滴度 (GMT) 是否不劣于三剂后的峰值抗 HPV16/18 GMT。
解释与创新
研究结果将支持在感染艾滋病毒的女性中实施减少剂量的方案,使 疫苗接种 更加可行,并有助于消除 宫颈癌,并加速全球健康公平。
科研与公卫意义
HPV 疫苗接种 对于预防 宫颈癌 非常有效,世界卫生组织目前建议免疫功能正常的女性注射一剂或两剂 HPV 疫苗接种 方案。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察性质和小样本量而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。该试验将招募来自加拿大五个省份 11 个地点的 275 名感染艾滋病毒的女性。

Rationale and design of a clinical trial of a reduced dosing schedule for the nonavalent human papillomavirus vaccine in women living with HIV: The NOVA-HIV study.

Authors: Emma Karlsen, Jenna Langlois, Isabelle Boucoiran, Jason Brophy, Chelsea Elwood, Catherine Hankins, Alicia Hornsberger, Mel Krajden, Marette Lee, Mona Loutfy
Original abstract (English)
Virtually all cases of cervical cancer are caused by human papillomavirus (HPV). Women and people with a cervix living with HIV (women with HIV) are disproportionately impacted by cervical cancer; they experience higher rates of HPV infection and increased susceptibility to persistent HPV infection than women without HIV. HPV vaccination is highly effective for prevention of cervical cancer, and the World Health Organization currently recommends a one or two-dose HPV vaccination schedule for immunocompetent females. Although the approved nonavalent HPV (9vHPV) vaccine is highly safe, immunogenic, and efficacious in those without HIV, there are very few data in women with HIV. To address this knowledge gap, the NOVA-HIV study is designed as a Canadian multicenter, observer-blind, randomized, phase IV clinical trial of the 9vHPV vaccine in women with HIV aged 18-45 years. The primary objective is to determine whether peak anti-HPV16/18 geometric mean titers (GMTs) after two doses of 9vHPV vaccine are non-inferior to peak anti-HPV16/18 GMTs after three doses. The trial will enroll 275 women with HIV from 11 sites across five Canadian provinces. Participants are randomized 1:1 to receive the 9vHPV vaccine at the routine schedule (0/2/6 months) or extended schedule (0/6/12 months). Over 24 months, participants attend six or seven visits, where clinical data, blood, and vaginal samples are collected. Data generated could support the implementation of reduced-dose schedules in women with HIV, making vaccination more feasible, contributing to the elimination of cervical cancer, and accelerating global health equity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to address the high rates of HPV infection and increased susceptibility to persistent HPV infection in women living with HIV, as these individuals are disproportionately impacted by cervical cancer. The study aimed to determine whether a reduced-dose schedule of the 9vHPV vaccine is non-inferior to the standard schedule in women with HIV.
Design and population
The NOVA-HIV study is a Canadian multicenter, observer-blind, randomized, phase IV clinical trial involving 275 women with HIV aged 18-45 years, randomized 1:1 to receive the 9vHPV vaccine at either the routine schedule (0/2/6 months) or extended schedule (0/6/12 months).
Core methods
Participants are randomized to receive the 9vHPV vaccine at the routine schedule (0/2/6 months) or extended schedule (0/6/12 months). Over 24 months, participants attend six or seven visits where clinical data, blood, and vaginal samples are collected.
Main results
The study aims to determine whether peak anti-HPV16/18 geometric mean titers (GMTs) after two doses of 9vHPV vaccine are non-inferior to peak anti-HPV16/18 GMTs after three doses.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings will support the implementation of reduced-dose schedules in women with HIV, making vaccination more feasible and contributing to the elimination of cervical cancer, and accelerating global health equity.
Scientific and public-health significance
HPV vaccination is highly effective for prevention of cervical cancer, and the World Health Organization currently recommends a one or two-dose HPV vaccination schedule for immunocompetent females.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and the small sample size, which may affect generalizability. The trial will enroll 275 women with HIV from 11 sites across five Canadian provinces.

人乳头瘤病毒 阳性女性中的局部羧甲基 β-葡聚糖和聚卡波非:一项前瞻性观察研究

Local carboxymethyl β-glucan and polycarbophil in women positive for human papillomavirus: a prospective observational study
作者： Carmen Pingarrón Santofimia, Ysmael Álvarez Espinosa, Juan José Hernández Aguado, Ana Isabel López Castejón, Maria Soledad Martínez García, Carlos Blanco-Soler, Cristina Del Valle Rubido, Maria del Pilar Robles Ruiz, Janet Abanto Alvarez, Maria del Carmen Chicharro Cassuso
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈细胞学检查正常或低级别的高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染通常通过观察进行管理,因为许多感染会自行清除。然而,持续感染,尤其是35岁以上的女性,可能会增加病情进展的风险,并导致监测延长、重复检查和患者焦虑。本研究评估了局部 治疗 与羧甲基 β-葡聚糖和聚卡波非 (Colpofix®) 在西班牙 HR-HPV 阳性女性群体中实现高危 (HR) 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 阴性的有效性。方法:这项前瞻性、非随机对照、多中心研究纳入了 533 名年龄在 25-65 岁之间、患有 HR-HPV 且细胞学正常、意义未明的非典型鳞状细胞或低度鳞状上皮内病变的女性。该研究在真实的临床环境中进行,采用为期 3 个月的 治疗 或“观望”方法,并在 6 个月时进行最终随访。统计分析包括各种检验,例如Mann-Whitney检验、Fisher精确检验和Pearson χ2检验。结果:6 个月时,50.1% 的治疗患者观察到 HR-HPV 阴性,而观察组的这一比例为 24.0% (p<0.0001)。所有 HR-HPV 基因型的清除率均较高。对于 HPV16,接受治疗的患者中有 50.7% 出现阴性,而对照组则有 9.2%(p=0.0291)。治疗 组的宫颈异位解决率更高(42.6% 对比 11.5%,p<0.0001)。没有不良事件的报告。结论:与 6 个月随访时单独观察相比,局部使用羧甲基 β-葡聚糖和聚卡波非 治疗 与较高的 HR-HPV 阴性率和异位消退率相关,支持其作为一种治疗选择,特别是在 35 岁以上的女性中。下载本文的简单语言摘要:https://www.drugsincontext.com/plain-language-summaries/plain-language-summary-local-carboxymethyl-β-glucan-and-polycarbophil-in-women-positive-for- human-papillomavirus-a-prospective-observational-study

设计与对象
这项前瞻性、非随机对照、多中心研究纳入了 533 名 25-65 岁女性,她们患有 HR-HPV 且细胞学正常、意义未明的非典型鳞状细胞或低度鳞状上皮内病变。
核心方法
统计分析包括各种检验,例如Mann-Whitney检验、Fisher精确检验和Pearson χ2检验。
主要结果
对于 HPV16,接受治疗的患者中有 50.7% 出现阴性,而对照组则有 9.2%(p=0.0291)。
解释与创新
没有不良事件的报告。
科研与公卫意义
然而,持续感染,尤其是35岁以上的女性,可能会增加病情进展的风险,并导致监测延长、重复检查和患者焦虑。
局限与证据强度
与 6 个月随访时单独观察相比,局部使用羧甲基 β-葡聚糖和聚卡波非 治疗 与较高的 HR-HPV 阴性率和异位消退率相关,支持其作为一种治疗选择,特别是在 35 岁以上的女性中。

Local carboxymethyl β-glucan and polycarbophil in women positive for human papillomavirus: a prospective observational study

Authors: Carmen Pingarrón Santofimia, Ysmael Álvarez Espinosa, Juan José Hernández Aguado, Ana Isabel López Castejón, Maria Soledad Martínez García, Carlos Blanco-Soler, Cristina Del Valle Rubido, Maria del Pilar Robles Ruiz, Janet Abanto Alvarez, Maria del Carmen Chicharro Cassuso
Original abstract (English)
Background: High-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infections with normal or low-grade cervical cytology are generally managed through observation because many infections clear spontaneously. However, persistent infection, particularly in women over 35 years of age, may increase the risk of progression and lead to prolonged surveillance, repeated examinations and patient anxiety. This study evaluates the effectiveness of local treatment with carboxymethyl β-glucan and polycarbophil (Colpofix®) in achieving negativization of high-risk (HR) human papillomavirus (HPV) in a Spanish population of women with HR-HPV positivity. Methods: This prospective, non-randomized controlled, multi-centre study enrolled 533 women aged 25–65 with HR-HPV and normal cytology, atypical squamous cells of undetermined significance or low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesions. Conducted in real clinical settings, it involved a 3-month treatment or ‘wait-and-see’ approach, with final follow-up at 6 months. Statistical analysis included various tests such as the Mann– Whitney test, Fisher’s exact test and Pearson’s χ2 test. Results: At 6 months, HR-HPV negativity was observed in 50.1% of treated patients compared with 24.0% in the observation group (p<0.0001). Higher clearance rates were recorded across all HR-HPV genotypes. For HPV16, negativization occurred in 50.7% of treated patients versus 9.2% of controls (p=0.0291). Resolution of cervical ectopy was more frequent in the treatment group (42.6% versus 11.5%, p<0.0001). No adverse events were reported. Conclusions: Local treatment with carboxymethyl β-glucan and polycarbophil was associated with higher rates of HR-HPV negativization and ectopy resolution compared with observation alone at 6-month follow-up, supporting its use as a management option, particularly in women over 35 years of age. 使用实时 PCR (Abbott m2000) 进行的女性宫颈标本中高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 基因型的分布,这是喀麦隆巴斯德中心五年来的回顾性分析

Distribution of high-risk Human Papillomavirus genotypes in cervical specimen from women using real-time PCR (Abbott m2000), a retrospective analysis over five years at Centre Pasteur du Cameroun
作者： Pretty Rosereine Mbouyap, Laure Ngono, Frederic Lissock, Thierry Noumsi, Ingrid Mouli, Romuald Boula, Ladifatou Nzi Mbouo Njoya, Melisa Awouapi, Clémentine Ndjitarai, Carine Waguia
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 人乳头瘤病毒(高风险,HR-HPV)是几乎所有 宫颈癌 的主要原因,其负担在撒哈拉以南非洲地区仍然特别高。本研究旨在描述 2021 年至 2025 年间转诊至喀麦隆巴斯德中心 (CPC) 的患者中的 患病率 和 HR-HPV 基因型分布,并评估年龄与所识别基因型之间的关联。方法对2021年1月1日至2025年12月31日CPC实验室信息系统(LIS)的电子记录进行横断面分析研究。采用实时PCR(Abbott m2000)对患者宫颈样本进行HR-HPV定性检测,检测14种基因型:16、18和其他12种高危基因型(31、33、 35、39、45、51、52、56、58、59、66、68)。使用 R 版本 4.0.5 进行分析,通过卡方检验评估分类年龄和基因型之间的关联,显着性阈值设置为 p

Distribution of high-risk Human Papillomavirus genotypes in cervical specimen from women using real-time PCR (Abbott m2000), a retrospective analysis over five years at Centre Pasteur du Cameroun

Authors: Pretty Rosereine Mbouyap, Laure Ngono, Frederic Lissock, Thierry Noumsi, Ingrid Mouli, Romuald Boula, Ladifatou Nzi Mbouo Njoya, Melisa Awouapi, Clémentine Ndjitarai, Carine Waguia
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background The human papillomavirus (high-risk, HR-HPV) is the leading cause of nearly all cervical cancers, whose burden remains particularly high in sub-Saharan Africa. This study aimed to describe the prevalence and distribution of HR-HPV genotypes among patients referred to the Centre Pasteur du Cameroun (CPC) between 2021 and 2025, and to assess the association between age and the genotype identified. Methods Analytical cross-sectional study conducted on electronic records from the CPC laboratory information system (LIS), from January 1, 2021 to December 31, 2025. Qualitative detection of HR-HPV was performed on patients' cervical samples by real-time PCR (Abbott m2000), detecting 14 genotypes: 16, 18, and twelve other high-risk genotypes grouped together (31, 33, 35, 39, 45, 51, 52, 56, 58, 59, 66, 68). Analyses were performed using R version 4.0.5, with a chi-square test to assess the association between categorized age and genotype, the significance threshold being set at p

一种 HPV16 VLP 构建和生产的新方法,具有改进的可及性和颗粒分选潜力

A novel approach to HPV16 VLP construction and production with improved accessibility and particle sorting potential
作者： Morgan R. Williamson, Caleb Boren, Rahul Kumar, Lauren S. Gollahon
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是乳头瘤病毒科的成员,可感染多种动物 宿主,并导致大多数 宫颈癌 和相当大比例的口咽癌。目前针对 HPV 相关宫颈疾病的 治疗 策略在很大程度上取决于疾病阶段,浸润前和早期浸润性病变通常通过手术切除或放射治疗。此外,更晚期的病例通常需要化疗、免疫疗法或联合疗法。预防性 疫苗接种 仍然是最有效的预防方法,目前可用的 疫苗,包括 Gardasil、Gardasil-9 和 Cervarix,依靠基于 L1 的病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 来诱导保护性免疫。与此同时,人们对使用纳米材料和病毒衣壳作为靶向药物和基因递送的平台越来越感兴趣,同时开发了用于纳米颗粒和病毒表征的高通量方法。修饰的 HPV 衣壳已显示出细胞特异性靶向和更有效的货物递送以及减少脱靶细胞毒性的前景。在这项研究中,使用专门设计的接头将 HPV16 结构蛋白 L1 和 L2 分别融合到金星荧光团的 N 端和 C 端,旨在保留衣壳组装,同时减少与当前生产方法相关的 VLP 聚集。由此产生的基于双分子荧光互补 (BiFC) 的 VLP 表现出聚集减少和总颗粒产量提高。此外,颗粒产生可检测的 YFP 信号,支持基于荧光的 VLP 组装监测,并为高通量颗粒表征和分选提供潜在基础。这种内在的荧光信号还可以减少下游分析对单独包装的报告构建体的依赖。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了改进 HPV16 VLP 生产方法的需求,以增强可及性和颗粒分选潜力,填补了当前 HPV16 VLP 构建和生产技术的空白。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用一种新方法设计和生产 HPV16 VLP,该方法在专门设计的接头中分别将 L1 和 L2 蛋白融合到金星荧光团的 N 端和 C 端。
核心方法
使用专门设计的接头将 HPV16 结构蛋白 L1 和 L2 融合到金星荧光团的 N 端和 C 端,以保留衣壳组装,同时减少 VLP 聚集。
主要结果
由此产生的 VLP 表现出聚集减少和总颗粒产量提高,并产生可检测的 YFP 信号,支持基于荧光的 VLP 组装监测。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为展示了一种 HPV16 VLP 构建和生产的新方法,具有改进的可及性和颗粒分选潜力,这可能对 HPV 研究和开发产生影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效、更容易获得的 HPV16 VLP 用于预防性 疫苗 和其他应用提供信息,从而有可能减少下游分析对单独包装的报告基因构建体的依赖。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括仅提供抽象证据,不提供详细的统计分析或比较数据,从而降低了人们对研究结果普遍性的信心。

A novel approach to HPV16 VLP construction and production with improved accessibility and particle sorting potential

Authors: Morgan R. Williamson, Caleb Boren, Rahul Kumar, Lauren S. Gollahon
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV), a member of the Papillomaviridae family, infects a broad range of animal hosts and is responsible for the majority of cervical cancers and a substantial proportion of oropharyngeal cancers. Current treatment strategies for HPV-associated cervical disease depend largely on disease stage, with preinvasive and early invasive lesions commonly managed through surgical excision or radiation. Additionally, more advanced cases often require chemotherapy, immunotherapy, or combination regimens. Prophylactic vaccination remains the most effective preventive approach, and currently available vaccines, including Gardasil, Gardasil-9, and Cervarix, rely on L1-based virus-like particles (VLPs) to induce protective immunity. At the same time, increasing interest has emerged in the use of nanomaterials and viral capsids as platforms for targeted drug and gene delivery, alongside the development of higher-throughput methods for nanoparticle and viral characterization. Modified HPV capsids have shown promise for cell-specific targeting and more efficient cargo delivery with reduced off-target cytotoxicity. In this study, the HPV16 structural proteins L1 and L2 were fused to the N- and C-terminal halves of the Venus fluorophore, respectively, using a specially designed linker intended to preserve capsid assembly while reducing VLP aggregation associated with current production methods. The resulting bimolecular fluorescence complementation (BiFC)-based VLPs demonstrated reduced aggregation and improved total particle yield. In addition, the particles generated a detectable YFP signal, supporting fluorescence-based monitoring of VLP assembly and providing a potential basis for high-throughput particle characterization and sorting. This intrinsic fluorescent signal may also reduce reliance on separately packaged reporter constructs for downstream analysis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for improved HPV16 VLP production methods to enhance accessibility and particle sorting potential, filling a gap in current HPV16 VLP construction and production techniques.
Design and population
The study involved the design and production of HPV16 VLPs using a novel approach that fused L1 and L2 proteins to the N- and C-terminal halves of the Venus fluorophore, respectively, in a specially designed linker.
Core methods
The HPV16 structural proteins L1 and L2 were fused to the N- and C-terminal halves of the Venus fluorophore using a specially designed linker to preserve capsid assembly while reducing VLP aggregation.
Main results
The resulting VLPs demonstrated reduced aggregation and improved total particle yield, and generated a detectable YFP signal, supporting fluorescence-based monitoring of VLP assembly.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating a novel approach to HPV16 VLP construction and production with improved accessibility and particle sorting potential, which could have implications for HPV research and development.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings could inform the development of more efficient and accessible HPV16 VLPs for use in prophylactic vaccines and other applications, potentially reducing reliance on separately packaged reporter constructs for downstream analysis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the abstract-only evidence, which does not provide detailed statistical analysis or comparative data, reducing confidence in the generalizability of the findings.

人乳头瘤病毒 筛查和自行采集的阴道样本。

Human Papillomavirus Screening and Self-Collected Vaginal Samples.
作者： Brianna Lambert, Jason T. Alexander
摘要中文翻译本 JAMA 临床指南概要总结了 2025 年关于 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查的建议以及持久共识 宫颈癌 筛查和管理指南委员会自行采集的阴道样本。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在总结2025年关于人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)筛查和自行采集阴道样本的建议,以解决有效的宫颈癌预防策略的科学和公共卫生问题。
设计与对象
本研究是持久共识 宫颈癌 筛查和管理指南委员会 2025 年建议的总结,重点是 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 筛查和在 宫颈癌 预防背景下自行采集的阴道样本。
核心方法
这些方法涉及审查和综合持久共识 宫颈癌 筛查和管理指南委员会的 2025 年建议,其中包括评估 HPV 筛查和自行收集的阴道样本在检测 HPV 相关疾病方面的功效的证据。
主要结果
该研究报告称,建议使用自行采集的阴道样本进行 HPV 筛查,作为检测与 宫颈癌 相关的 高危型HPV 类型的有效方法,特别强调 HPV16 和 HPV18。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为支持使用自行采集的阴道样本进行 HPV 筛查,强调了它们在提高可及性和遵守 宫颈癌 筛查方案方面的作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过推广使用自行采集的阴道样本进行 HPV 筛查来为公共卫生策略提供信息,这可能会加强不同人群的 宫颈癌 预防工作。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于现有指南的总结,这可能会限制分析的深度,但由于其与既定建议的一致性,证据被认为是中等的。

Human Papillomavirus Screening and Self-Collected Vaginal Samples.

Authors: Brianna Lambert, Jason T. Alexander
Original abstract (English)
This JAMA Clinical Guidelines Synopsis summarizes the 2025 recommendations on human papillomavirus (HPV) screening and self-collected vaginal samples from the Enduring Consensus Cervical Cancer Screening and Management Guidelines Committee.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to summarize the 2025 recommendations on human papillomavirus (HPV) screening and self-collected vaginal samples to address the scientific and public health problem of effective cervical cancer prevention strategies.
Design and population
This study is a summary of the 2025 recommendations from the Enduring Consensus Cervical Cancer Screening and Management Guidelines Committee, focusing on human papillomavirus (HPV) screening and self-collected vaginal samples in the context of cervical cancer prevention.
Core methods
The methods involved reviewing and synthesizing the 2025 recommendations from the Enduring Consensus Cervical Cancer Screening and Management Guidelines Committee, which included evaluating evidence on HPV screening and self-collected vaginal samples for their efficacy in detecting HPV-associated diseases.
Main results
The study reported that HPV screening using self-collected vaginal samples is recommended as an effective method for detecting high-risk HPV types associated with cervical cancer, with specific emphasis on HPV16 and HPV18.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as supporting the use of self-collected vaginal samples for HPV screening, highlighting their role in improving accessibility and adherence to cervical cancer screening protocols.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies by promoting the use of self-collected vaginal samples for HPV screening, which may enhance cervical cancer prevention efforts in diverse populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a summary of existing guidelines, which may limit the depth of analysis, but the evidence is considered moderate due to its alignment with established recommendations.

女性高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 感染与阴道微生物群失调之间的关联

Association Between High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection and Vaginal Microbiota Dysbiosis Among Women
作者： Bhagyashri Patil-Takbhate, Priyanka Jali, Ajay Koli, Poonam Suryawanshi, Manjusha S. Deshpande, Bhagyashri Patil‐Takbhate
摘要中文翻译背景:宫颈癌 仍然是印度女性癌症相关死亡的主要原因之一。它是由高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 型持续感染引起,并受到菌群失调的影响。识别HPV基因型是准确评估风险、制定针对性防控策略的第一步。由于 HPV 疫苗 并不能完全预防所有可能导致 宫颈癌 的 HPV 毒株,因此定期筛查仍然有必要​‍​‌‍​‍‌​‍​‌‍​‍‌。材料和方法:一项在医院进行的横断面研究,纳入了 150 名出现阴道分泌物异常的育龄妇女。收集了人口统计和生殖信息。使用培养物和 16S rRNA Illumina MiSeq 测序分析阴道拭子的微生物群。使用液基细胞学 (LBC) 试剂盒收集宫颈拭子,进行 HPV 基因分型和细胞学分析。采用t检验、χ2检验、k均值聚类分析进行统计分析;P<0.05被认为具有统计学意义。结果:女性HR-HPV 患病率率为13.33%;3个样本显示多基因型感染。女性平均年龄为39.66岁。最常检测到的基因型是 HPV 68 和 59。HPV 阳性和 HSIL 样本表现出增加的微生物多样性,包括加德纳菌、普雷沃菌和迪亚利斯特菌等厌氧菌的富集。结论:在这个以医院为基础的有症状女性样本中,阴道生态失调与 HR-HPV 感染相关,尽管这一发现受到 HPV 阳性病例数量较少的限制。恢复健康的阴道微生物群与基因型特异性 HPV 疫苗接种 相结合是否可以减轻 HPV 相关疾病的负担仍有待测试。需要纵向和基因型特异性分析研究来阐明因果机制和区域差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查女性高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (HR-HPV) 感染与阴道微生物群失调之间的关系,满足更好地了解 HPV 相关疾病机制并制定有针对性的预防策略的需要。
设计与对象
一家医院对 150 名出现阴道分泌物异常的育龄妇女进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
使用培养物和 16S rRNA Illumina MiSeq 测序分析阴道拭子的微生物群,同时使用液基细胞学 (LBC) 试剂盒收集宫颈拭子进行 HPV 基因分型和细胞学分析。使用t检验、χ2检验和k均值聚类分析进行统计分析。
主要结果
女性中 HR-HPV 的 患病率 率为 13.33%,其中 HPV 68 和 59 是最常检测到的基因型。HPV 阳性和 HSIL 样本表现出增加的微生物多样性,包括加德纳菌、普雷沃菌和迪亚利斯特菌等厌氧菌的富集。
解释与创新
研究发现,阴道生态失调与 HR-HPV 感染有关,尽管这一发现受到 HPV 阳性病例数量较少的限制。作者认为,恢复健康的阴道微生物群,结合基因型特异性 HPV 疫苗接种,可以减轻 HPV 相关疾病的负担。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对阴道微生物群可能是减少 HPV 相关疾病负担的潜在策略,尽管需要进一步的纵向和基因型特异性研究来阐明因果机制和区域差异。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到 HPV 阳性病例数量较少的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。作者建议需要进一步研究来证实这些发现。

Association Between High-Risk Human Papillomavirus Infection and Vaginal Microbiota Dysbiosis Among Women

Authors: Bhagyashri Patil-Takbhate, Priyanka Jali, Ajay Koli, Poonam Suryawanshi, Manjusha S. Deshpande, Bhagyashri Patil‐Takbhate
Original abstract (English)
Background: Cervical cancer is still one of the major causes of cancer-related deaths among women in India. It is caused by a persistent infection with a high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) type, influenced by dysbiosis. Identifying the HPV genotypes is the primary step in accurately assessing risk and developing targeted prevention and control strategies. As the HPV vaccine is not a complete protection against all HPV strains that may cause cervical cancer, regular screening is still ​‍​‌‍​‍‌​‍​‌‍​‍‌necessary. Materials and Methods: A cross-sectional study done at a hospital included 150 women of reproductive age presenting with abnormal vaginal discharge. Demographic and reproductive information were collected. Vaginal swabs were analyzed for microbiota using culture and 16S rRNA Illumina MiSeq sequencing. Cervical swabs were collected using a liquid-based cytology (LBC) kit for HPV genotyping and cytology. Statistical analysis was performed using t tests, χ2 tests, and k-means cluster analysis; P <0.05 was considered statistically significant. Results: The prevalence of HR-HPV was 13.33% among women; 3 samples showed multiple-genotype infection. The mean age of women was 39.66 years. The most frequently detected genotypes were HPV 68 and 59. HPV-positive and HSIL samples exhibited increased microbial diversity, including enrichment of anaerobic bacteria such as Gardnerella, Prevotella, and Dialister . Conclusions: In this hospital-based sample of symptomatic women, vaginal dysbiosis was associated with HR-HPV infection, although this finding is limited by the small number of HPV-positive cases. Whether restoring a healthy vaginal microbiota, in combination with genotype-specific HPV vaccination, could reduce the burden of HPV-related disease remains to be tested. Longitudinal and genotype-specific analytical studies are needed to elucidate causal mechanisms and regional variations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the association between high-risk human papillomavirus (HR-HPV) infection and vaginal microbiota dysbiosis among women, addressing the need for better understanding of HPV-related disease mechanisms and developing targeted prevention strategies.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted at a hospital involving 150 women of reproductive age presenting with abnormal vaginal discharge.
Core methods
Vaginal swabs were analyzed for microbiota using culture and 16S rRNA Illumina MiSeq sequencing, while cervical swabs were collected for HPV genotyping and cytology using a liquid-based cytology (LBC) kit. Statistical analysis was performed using t tests, χ2 tests, and k-means cluster analysis.
Main results
The prevalence of HR-HPV was 13.33% among women, with HPV 68 and 59 being the most frequently detected genotypes. HPV-positive and HSIL samples exhibited increased microbial diversity, including enrichment of anaerobic bacteria such as Gardnerella, Prevotella, and Dialister.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that vaginal dysbiosis was associated with HR-HPV infection, although this finding is limited by the small number of HPV-positive cases. The authors suggest that restoring a healthy vaginal microbiota, in combination with genotype-specific HPV vaccination, could reduce the burden of HPV-related disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting the vaginal microbiota could be a potential strategy for reducing HPV-related disease burden, although further longitudinal and genotype-specific studies are needed to elucidate causal mechanisms and regional variations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the small number of HPV-positive cases, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The authors suggest that further research is needed to confirm these findings.

喀麦隆 COVID-19 大流行之前、期间和之后 宫颈癌 筛查参与、障碍、HPV 感染和细胞病理学特征的时间趋势

Temporal trends in cervical cancer screening participation, barriers, HPV infection, and cytopathological profiles before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic in Cameroon
作者： Embolo Enyegue Elisée Libert, Abe Ngono Osvalde, Evina Nlatte Marlyse Tabita, Awalou Halidou, NGONO ABONDO Floride Enstelle, Emvoutou Maboulou Jeanne Valerie, Halmata Mohamadou, Ngoutane Aicha, Kalla Ange Danielle, Roch Bredin Bissala Nkounkou
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 宫颈癌 仍然是喀麦隆的一项重大公共卫生挑战,该国筛查的普及率有限且获取机会不平等。本研究评估了 宫颈癌 筛查、HPV 感染、细胞病理学结果以及在 COVID-19 大流行之前、期间和之后与不参与相关的因素的时间趋势。方法进行了一项连续横断面研究,纳入 2016 年至 2024 年间从喀麦隆城乡卫生机构招募的 3,751 名 25-65 岁女性。使用结构化问卷收集社会人口、行为、社会文化、医疗保健相关和 COVID-19 相关数据。使用 Bethesda 系统和 HPV 基因分型对宫颈样本进行细胞学检查。对大流行前(2016-2019)、大流行(2020-2021)和大流行后(2022-2024)时期的时间模式进行了检查。使用逻辑回归和多重对应分析来确定与不参与筛查相关的因素。结果 在大流行期间筛查率下降,随后又上升,但农村女性的筛查率始终低于城市女性。HPV 患病率 在研究期间也有所下降,其中 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 是最常检测到的基因型。LSIL 和 HSIL 分别在 9.0% 和 4.0% 的参与者中观察到。不参与与居住在农村、缺乏健康保险、害怕妇科检查、偏爱传统医学以及害怕接触 COVID-19 相关。结论 喀麦隆的 宫颈癌 筛查受到相互关联的地理、社会经济、社会文化和卫生系统障碍的影响。加强分散的、负担得起的、文化上适当的筛查服务至关重要,特别是对于农村和弱势群体而言。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估喀麦隆 COVID-19 大流行之前、期间和之后 宫颈癌 筛查、HPV 感染和细胞病理学结果的时间趋势,解决筛查普及有限和机会不平等的公共卫生挑战。
设计与对象
2016 年至 2024 年间,对喀麦隆城乡卫生机构的 3,751 名 25-65 岁女性进行了一系列横断面研究,评估了与不参加筛查相关的社会人口、行为、社会文化、医疗保健相关和 COVID-19 相关因素。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集社会人口学、行为、社会文化、医疗保健相关和 COVID-19 相关数据,并使用 Bethesda 系统和 HPV 基因分型检查宫颈样本。使用逻辑回归和多重对应分析来识别与不参与相关的因素。
主要结果
筛查率在大流行期间下降,随后又上升,但农村妇女的筛查率始终较低。HPV 患病率 在研究期间有所下降,其中 HPV-16 和 HPV-18 是最常检测到的基因型。LSIL 和 HSIL 分别在 9.0% 和 4.0% 的参与者中观察到。
解释与创新
研究发现,不参加 宫颈癌 筛查与农村居住、缺乏健康保险、害怕妇科检查、偏爱传统医学以及害怕接触 COVID-19 相关暴露有关,这凸显了对文化上适当和分散的筛查服务的需求。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,加强分散、负担得起且文化上适当的筛查服务对于提高 宫颈癌 筛查率和早期发现至关重要,特别是对于农村和弱势群体。
局限与证据强度
该研究的设计局限性包括潜在的回忆偏差和缺乏随机性,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

Temporal trends in cervical cancer screening participation, barriers, HPV infection, and cytopathological profiles before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic in Cameroon

Authors: Embolo Enyegue Elisée Libert, Abe Ngono Osvalde, Evina Nlatte Marlyse Tabita, Awalou Halidou, NGONO ABONDO Floride Enstelle, Emvoutou Maboulou Jeanne Valerie, Halmata Mohamadou, Ngoutane Aicha, Kalla Ange Danielle, Roch Bredin Bissala Nkounkou
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Cervical cancer remains a major public health challenge in Cameroon, where screening uptake is limited and access is unequal. This study assessed temporal trends in cervical cancer screening, HPV infection, cytopathological findings, and factors associated with non-participation before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic. Methods A serial cross-sectional study included 3,751 women aged 25–65 years recruited from urban and rural health facilities in Cameroon between 2016 and 2024. Sociodemographic, behavioral, sociocultural, healthcare-related, and COVID-19-related data were collected using structured questionnaires. Cervical samples underwent cytological examination using the Bethesda system and HPV genotyping. Temporal patterns were examined across pre-pandemic (2016–2019), pandemic (2020–2021), and post-pandemic (2022–2024) periods. Logistic regression and multiple correspondence analysis were used to identify factors associated with non-participation in screening. Results Screening uptake declined during the pandemic period and subsequently increased, while remaining consistently lower among rural than urban women. HPV prevalence also declined over the study period, with HPV-16 and HPV-18 among the most frequently detected genotypes. LSIL and HSIL were observed in 9.0% and 4.0% of participants, respectively. Non-participation was associated with rural residence, lack of health insurance, fear of gynecological examination, preference for traditional medicine, and fear of COVID-19-related exposure. Conclusion Cervical cancer screening in Cameroon is shaped by interconnected geographic, socioeconomic, sociocultural, and health-system barriers. Strengthening decentralized, affordable, culturally appropriate screening services is essential, particularly for rural and vulnerable populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess temporal trends in cervical cancer screening, HPV infection, and cytopathological findings before, during, and after the COVID-19 pandemic in Cameroon, addressing the public health challenge of limited screening uptake and unequal access.
Design and population
A serial cross-sectional study of 3,751 women aged 25–65 years was conducted in urban and rural health facilities in Cameroon between 2016 and 2024, assessing sociodemographic, behavioral, sociocultural, healthcare-related, and COVID-19-related factors associated with non-participation in screening.
Core methods
Sociodemographic, behavioral, sociocultural, healthcare-related, and COVID-19-related data were collected using structured questionnaires, and cervical samples were examined using the Bethesda system and HPV genotyping. Logistic regression and multiple correspondence analysis were used to identify factors associated with non-participation.
Main results
Screening uptake declined during the pandemic period and subsequently increased, remaining consistently lower among rural women. HPV prevalence decreased over the study period, with HPV-16 and HPV-18 being the most frequently detected genotypes. LSIL and HSIL were observed in 9.0% and 4.0% of participants, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that non-participation in cervical cancer screening was associated with rural residence, lack of health insurance, fear of gynecological examination, preference for traditional medicine, and fear of COVID-19-related exposure, highlighting the need for culturally appropriate and decentralized screening services.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that strengthening decentralized, affordable, and culturally appropriate screening services is essential, particularly for rural and vulnerable populations, to improve cervical cancer screening uptake and early detection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations include potential recall bias and the lack of randomization, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

埃塞俄比亚西北部因吉巴拉镇家长为女儿接种 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗的意愿及相关因素

Parental willingness to vaccinate daughters against human papillomavirus and associated factors in Injibara Town, Northwest Ethiopia
作者： Mekuanint Taddele Tessema, Daniel Adane Endalew, Wolde Melese Ayele, Ayenew Takele Alemu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 2025 年埃塞俄比亚西北部 Injibara 镇的父母为女儿接种 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗的意愿及相关因素。
设计与对象
对于定量和定性研究,分别使用简单随机和有目的抽样招募了 403 名和 12 名参与者。
核心方法
采用多变量二元逻辑回归来识别与结果变量相关的因素。
主要结果
使用调整后的奇数比和相应的 95% 置信区间来衡量关联强度,并在 p 值小于 0.05 时宣布统计关联。
解释与创新
大约四分之三的父母愿意为女儿接种 HPV 疫苗,与其他研究结果相比,这一结果还算乐观。
科研与公卫意义
摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 目前被推荐作为 宫颈癌 的预防策略。
局限与证据强度
定性研究补充了这些发现,揭示了错误信息,例如对不孕不育的恐惧、知识不足以及对学校项目之外的 疫苗 网站的访问有限等障碍。

Parental willingness to vaccinate daughters against human papillomavirus and associated factors in Injibara Town, Northwest Ethiopia

Authors: Mekuanint Taddele Tessema, Daniel Adane Endalew, Wolde Melese Ayele, Ayenew Takele Alemu
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is currently recommended as prevention strategy for cervical cancer. Parental permission is significantly important to increase HPV vaccination coverage among target daughters. This study aimed to assess parental willingness to vaccinate daughters against human papillomavirus and associated factors in Injibara town, Northwest Ethiopia, 2025. A Community-based cross-sectional convergent mixed-methods study was conducted from February 20 to March 20, 2025. For quantitative and qualitative studies, 403 and 12 participants were recruited using simple random and purposive samplings, respectively. An interviewer-administered structured questionnaire tool was used to collect quantitative data. Multi-variable binary logistics regression was employed to identify factors associated with the outcome variable. An adjusted odd ratio with the corresponding 95% confidence interval was used to measure strengths of associations and statistical associations were declared at p-values less than 0.05. Qualitative data were collected using topic guide by in-depth interview technique and were coded, transcribed verbatim, and analysed thematically. The overall parental willingness to vaccinate daughters against HPV was found to be 75.7% (95% CI: 71.2%-79.9%). Daughters’ perceived health status (AOR = 4.51, 95% CI: 2.25-9.00), childhood vaccination (AOR = 2.05, 95% CI: 1.19–3.53), knowledge on HPV (AOR = 2.27, 95% CI: 1.18–4.37), and attitude (AOR = 2.15, 95% CI: 1.23–3.77) were factors associated. Misconceptions on vaccine side effects, information gaps and limited access to additional vaccination sites were barriers of willingness to vaccinate daughters. Approximately three-quarters of parents were willing to vaccinate daughters against HPV which is moderately promising compared with other findings. Daughters’ perceived health status, childhood vaccination, knowledge, and attitude on HPV were significantly associated factors. Qualitative study complemented these findings by revealing misinformation like fear of infertility, inadequate knowledge, and limited access to vaccine sites outside of school-based programs as barriers. The results collectively suggest that parental willingness to vaccinate daughters against HPV could be enhanced through improving parental attitudes and knowledge, addressing myths through reliable means of communication, and expanding vaccination sites outside of school settings.

Scientific and public-health significance
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination is currently recommended as prevention strategy for cervical cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength

具有不同临床结果的口咽鳞状细胞癌中的 HPV16 转录模式

HPV16 transcriptional patterns in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma with different clinical outcomes
作者： Sadaf Sakina Hassan, Mehran Ghaderi, Linda Marklund, Pedro Farrajota Neves da Silva, Laila Sara Arroyo Mühr, Anders Näsman
摘要中文翻译摘要 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 16 是许多西方国家口咽鳞状细胞癌 (OPSCC) 的主要原因。尽管 HPV16 阳性 OPSCC 患者通常比 HPV 阴性患者有更好的预后,但存在显着的异质性,并且这些患者中的一部分仍然预后不良。病毒基因表达模式对这种异质性的贡献仍不清楚。在这项研究中,对来自 50 名 HPV 阳性 OPSCC 患者的激光捕获显微切割存档肿瘤样本进行了深度 RNA 测序,这些患者的临床结果已知(29 名预后良好,21 名预后不良)。RNA测序证实了40例HPV16(22例预后良好,18例预后不良),这些病例被纳入HPV16特异性转录分析。另外评估残留微生物读数作为质量控制措施。在预后不良组中观察到较高的相对 E6 表达以及更频繁地缺失 L1 和 E5 转录本。这些关联在初步分析中名义上是显着的,但在错误发现率校正后并没有保持统计显着性。在根据晚期淋巴结分期调整的探索性回归分析中,较高的相对 E6 表达以及 E5 和 L1 的缺失名义上仍然与不良预后相关。敏感性分析排除了 25% HPV 总读数最低的 HPV16 阳性样本,加强了观察到的 E6 差异,但减弱了 E5 和 L1 转录本缺失的关联。总体而言,这项探索性研究确定了具有不同临床结果的患者的 OPSCC 肿瘤之间的 HPV16 转录差异。这些发现是假设生成,需要在更大、独立的 队列研究 中进行验证,然后才能推断出预后或临床相关性。

科研与公卫意义
另外评估残留微生物读数作为质量控制措施。
局限与证据强度
尽管 HPV16 阳性 OPSCC 患者通常比 HPV 阴性患者有更好的预后,但存在显着的异质性,并且这些患者中的一部分仍然预后不良。

HPV16 transcriptional patterns in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma with different clinical outcomes

Authors: Sadaf Sakina Hassan, Mehran Ghaderi, Linda Marklund, Pedro Farrajota Neves da Silva, Laila Sara Arroyo Mühr, Anders Näsman
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Human papillomavirus (HPV) 16 is the leading cause of oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma (OPSCC) in many Western countries. Although patients with HPV16-positive OPSCC generally have better outcomes than those with HPV-negative disease, significant heterogeneity exists, and a subset of these patients still experience poor prognosis. The contribution of viral gene expression patterns to this heterogeneity remains unclear. In this study, deep RNA sequencing was performed on laser-capture microdissected archival tumor samples from 50 HPV-positive OPSCC patients with known clinical outcomes (29 good prognosis, 21 poor prognosis). RNA sequencing confirmed HPV16 in 40 cases (22 good prognosis, 18 poor prognosis), which were included in the HPV16-specific transcriptional analyses. Residual microbial reads were additionally assessed as a quality-control measure. Higher relative E6 expression and more frequent absence of L1 and E5 transcripts were observed in the poor-prognosis group. These associations were nominally significant in the primary analyses but did not remain statistically significant after false discovery rate correction. In exploratory regression analyses adjusted for advanced nodal stage, higher relative E6 expression and absence of E5 and L1 remained nominally associated with poor prognosis. Sensitivity analysis excluding the 25% of HPV16-positive samples with the lowest total HPV read counts strengthened the observed E6 difference but attenuated the associations for E5 and L1 transcript absence. Overall, this exploratory study identifies HPV16 transcriptional differences between OPSCC tumors from patients with different clinical outcomes. These findings are hypothesis-generating and require validation in larger, independent cohorts before prognostic or clinical relevance can be inferred.

Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

尼泊尔帕罗帕卡妇产医院患者中 人乳头瘤病毒 的频率

Frequency of Human Papillomavirus in Patients Attending Paropakar Maternityand Women’s Hospital, Nepal
作者： Minaxi Thakur, Snigdha Rai, Sristee Prajapati, Bibek Bhattarai, Sweta Silwal
摘要中文翻译引言:宫颈癌仍然是尼泊尔发病率和死亡率的主要原因。人乳头瘤病毒(人乳头瘤病毒)感染是主要的病因。本研究旨在确定在加德满都三级妇产科门诊就诊的女性中人乳头瘤病毒感染的频率。方法:在帕罗帕卡尔妇产医院进行了为期三个月的横断面研究。共纳入185名30至60岁之间的已婚或有性生活的女性。收集宫颈样本并检测人乳头瘤病毒DNA,包括高危型病毒HPV-16和HPV-18。同时收集了社会人口学和临床数据。使用SPSS v26进行数据分析,采用卡方检验和方差分析,p < 0.05被认为具有统计学意义。结果:在185名女性中,172名(93.0%)为HPV阴性。13名(7.0%)为HPV阳性:其中7名(3.8%)为HPV-16阳性,3名(1.6%)为HPV-18阳性,其余3名(1.6%)为其他类型。HPV阳性在50至59岁女性中更为常见。我们发现绝经后女性比绝经前女性更有可能感染HPV。未发现与生育次数、吸烟或初婚年龄有显著关联。结论:7%的女性检测出HPV感染,其中HPV-16是最常见的基因型。这些发现强调了在尼泊尔加强筛查和疫苗接种项目的重要性。

问题与背景
宫颈癌 仍然是尼泊尔发病率和 死亡率 的主要原因。
设计与对象
帕罗帕卡妇产医院进行了为期三个月的横断面研究。
核心方法
收集宫颈样本并检测 HPV DNA,包括高危基因型 HPV-16 和 HPV-18。
主要结果
我们发现绝经后女性比绝经前女性更容易感染 HPV。
解释与创新
没有发现与胎次、吸烟或结婚年龄有显着关联。
科研与公卫意义
7% 的人检测到 HPV,其中 HPV-16 是最常见的基因型。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Frequency of Human Papillomavirus in Patients Attending Paropakar Maternityand Women’s Hospital, Nepal

Authors: Minaxi Thakur, Snigdha Rai, Sristee Prajapati, Bibek Bhattarai, Sweta Silwal
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Cervical cancer remains a major cause of morbidity and mortality in Nepal. Human Papillomavirus (HPV) infection is the primary etiological factor. This study aimed to determine the frequency of HPV infection among women attending a tertiary care gynecological outpatient department in Kathmandu. Methods: A cross-sectional study was conducted at Paropakar Maternity and Women’s Hospital over three months. A total of 185 married or sexually active women aged 30–60 years were enrolled. Cervical samples were collected and tested for HPV DNA, including high-risk genotypes HPV-16 and HPV-18. Sociodemographic and clinical data were collected. Data analysis was performed using SPSS v26, applying chi-square and ANOVA tests, with p < 0.05 considered significant. Results: Of 185 women, 172 (93.0%) were HPV negative. Thirteen (7.0%) tested positive: HPV-16 in 7 (3.8%), HPV-18 in 3 (1.6%), and other types in 3 (1.6%). HPV positivity was more frequent in women aged 50–59 years. We found that postmenopausal women were significantly more likely to have HPV infection than premenopausal women. No significant associations were found with parity, smoking, or age at marriage. Conclusions: HPV was detected in 7%, with HPV-16 being the most common genotype. These findings underscore the importance of strengthening screening and vaccination programs in Nepal.

Cervical cancer remains a major cause of morbidity and mortality in Nepal.
Design and population
A cross-sectional study was conducted at Paropakar Maternity and Women’s Hospital over three months.
Core methods
Cervical samples were collected and tested for HPV DNA, including high-risk genotypes HPV-16 and HPV-18.
Main results
We found that postmenopausal women were significantly more likely to have HPV infection than premenopausal women.
Interpretation and novelty
No significant associations were found with parity, smoking, or age at marriage.
Scientific and public-health significance
HPV was detected in 7%, with HPV-16 being the most common genotype.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

高危型HPV DNA 和细胞学在诊断癌前和早期恶性宫颈病变中的诊断准确性:系统评价和荟萃分析方案

Diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA and cytology in diagnosing precancerous and early malignant cervical lesions: a protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis
作者： Shrish Vijayakumar Raut, Mallika Sengupta, Pradeep S. Anand, Balasubramanian Ganesh, Joshua Chadwick, Ramanandvignesh Pandiyan, Kavitha P. Kamath, Vignes Anand Srinivasalu, Adhin Bhaskar, Jefferson Daniel J
摘要中文翻译高危型hr-HPV持续感染是宫颈癌发病的主要原因。通过筛查早期发现是最好的预防策略。hr-HPV DNA 和巴氏涂片这两种主要筛查技术针对 宫颈癌 自然进展中两个不同时间点的疾病状况,一个分别在初始阶段,另一个在细胞学变化水平。我们的目标是确定 hr-HPV DNA 检测的诊断准确性,并将其与巴氏涂片和联合检测在检测早期癌症病变方面的诊断准确性进行比较。我们将搜索包括 MEDLINE、Embase、Cochrane 和 Scopus 在内的电子数据库,以查找报告单独使用 hr-HPV DNA 检测或与巴氏涂片联合检测对接受 宫颈癌 筛查的任何年龄女性的诊断准确性的研究。我们将纳入根据组织病理学检查评估 WHO 预审 HPV-DNA 检测癌前病变 (CIN1-3) 和早期恶性宫颈病变 (FIGO IA 期) 的研究。两名审稿人将独立筛选符合条件的文章,我们将使用 QUADAS-2 和 QUADAS-C 评估方法学质量。双变量模型将用于荟萃分析,以尽可能估计汇总敏感性和特异性。将使用诊断测试准确性研究的 GRADE 方法评估证据的确定性。我们预计数据、设置和方法会出现偏差、挑战和异质性。然而,这项工作旨在提供更多关于 宫颈癌 hr-HPV DNA 检测诊断准确性的信息,支持循证预防和早期检测策略。普洛斯彼罗 CRD420251251393。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在确定 高危型HPV DNA 检测在检测早期癌症病变中的诊断准确性,满足对该方法相对于巴氏涂片和联合检测的有效性的更多信息的需求。
设计与对象
将使用根据组织病理学检查评估 WHO 资格预审的 HPV-DNA 的研究,进行系统回顾和荟萃分析,以评估 高危型HPV DNA 检测在检测癌前和早期恶性宫颈病变方面的诊断准确性。
核心方法
研究将通过搜索电子数据库来确定,并使用 QUADAS-2 和 QUADAS-C 评估方法学质量。双变量模型将用于荟萃分析,证据的确定性将使用 GRADE 方法进行评估。
主要结果
该研究将评估 高危型HPV DNA 检测在检测早期癌症病变方面的诊断准确性,并将其与巴氏涂片和联合检测进行比较。
解释与创新
这项工作旨在提供更多有关 高危型HPV DNA 检测 宫颈癌 诊断准确性的信息,支持循证预防和早期检测策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果将为监测、诊断以及宫颈癌的早期检测策略提供信息,为公共卫生工作做出贡献。
局限与证据强度
由于数据异质性,该研究可能面临偏见和挑战,但其目的是为 高危型HPV DNA 测试的诊断准确性提供有力的证据。

Diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA and cytology in diagnosing precancerous and early malignant cervical lesions: a protocol for a systematic review and meta-analysis

Authors: Shrish Vijayakumar Raut, Mallika Sengupta, Pradeep S. Anand, Balasubramanian Ganesh, Joshua Chadwick, Ramanandvignesh Pandiyan, Kavitha P. Kamath, Vignes Anand Srinivasalu, Adhin Bhaskar, Jefferson Daniel J
Original abstract (English)
Persistent infection with high-risk hr-HPV is a major cause in the aetiopathogenesis of cervical cancer. Early detection by screening is the best strategy for prevention. Two major screening techniques hr-HPV DNA and Pap smear, target the disease condition at two different timepoints in the natural progression of cervical cancer, one at the initial stage and the other at the level of cytological changes, respectively. Our objectives are to determine the diagnostic accuracy of hr-HPV DNA testing and compare it with that of Pap smear and co-testing in detecting early cancer lesions. We will search electronic databases, including MEDLINE, Embase, Cochrane, and Scopus for studies that reported the diagnostic accuracy of hr-HPV DNA testing alone or in combination with Pap in women of any age undergoing cervical cancer screening. We will include studies that evaluated WHO-prequalified HPV-DNA for the detection of precancerous (CIN1–3) and early malignant cervical lesions (FIGO Stage IA) against histopathological examination. Two reviewers will independently screen the eligible articles, and we will assess methodological quality using QUADAS-2 and QUADAS-C. A bivariate model will be used for meta-analysis to estimate summary sensitivity and specificity wherever possible. Certainty of evidence will be assessed using the GRADE approach for diagnostic test accuracy studies. We anticipate biases, challenges, heterogeneity from data, settings, and methods. However, the work aims to provide more information on the diagnostic accuracy of hr-HPV DNA tests for cervical cancer, supporting evidence-based prevention and early detection strategies. PROSPERO CRD420251251393.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to determine the diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA testing in detecting early cancer lesions, addressing the need for more information on this method's effectiveness compared to Pap smear and co-testing.
Design and population
A systematic review and meta-analysis will be conducted to evaluate the diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA testing in detecting precancerous and early malignant cervical lesions, using studies that evaluated WHO-prequalified HPV-DNA against histopathological examination.
Core methods
Studies will be identified through searches of electronic databases, and methodological quality will be assessed using QUADAS-2 and QUADAS-C. A bivariate model will be used for meta-analysis, and certainty of evidence will be assessed using the GRADE approach.
Main results
The study will assess the diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA testing in detecting early cancer lesions, comparing it with Pap smear and co-testing.
Interpretation and novelty
The work aims to provide more information on the diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA tests for cervical cancer, supporting evidence-based prevention and early detection strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings will inform surveillance, diagnosis, and early detection strategies for cervical cancer, contributing to public health efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study may face biases and challenges due to data heterogeneity, but it aims to provide robust evidence on the diagnostic accuracy of high-risk HPV DNA tests.
