A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

赞比亚农村地区家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗 的看法和接受度:卡邦波医院附属健康中心的描述性现象学研究

Parents perceptions and acceptability of the human papillomavirus vaccine in rural Zambia: a descriptive phenomenological study at Kabompo Hospital Affiliated Health Centre
作者： Sondashi Bombwe, Sebean Mayimbo, Stella Nankamba
摘要中文翻译宫颈癌 是赞比亚妇女癌症死亡的主要原因,针对 9-14 岁女孩的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是主要的一级预防策略。由于 疫苗 是为未成年人提供的,因此使用取决于父母的同意,而父母的同意取决于父母对 疫苗 的看法。在西北省的一个多民族农村地区 Kabompo,到 2024 年,只有 59% 的符合条件的女孩接种了疫苗,远低于全球目标,但没有定性证据表明此类环境中的父母如何理解和决定 疫苗。2025 年 6 月至 8 月,我们在 Kabompo 医院附属健康中心进行了一项描述性现象学研究。采用最大变异抽样方法有目的地选择 20 名 9-14 岁女孩的父母和法定监护人,并用英语或卢瓦莱语进行深度访谈。访谈内容均进行录音、逐字转录,并使用 Colaizzi 的七步法和 QDA Miner Lite 进行分析。产生了五个主题:HPV 及其 疫苗 的知识和意识;对安全性和有效性的看法;社会文化和宗教影响;卫生工作者的作用;和准入障碍。大多数父母都知道 疫苗 可以预防 宫颈癌,但很少有人能够解释 HPV 如何导致癌症或说明其时间表,并且有一些父母在服务点首次了解 疫苗。对不孕不育的恐惧是最突出的担忧,此外还有对 疫苗 太新的不安。神圣保护的宗教框架、对草药的偏爱以及对 疫苗接种 纵容早期性行为的担忧影响了我们的决定。卫生工作者是最值得信赖的信息来源,而且往往具有决定性作用。距离、运输成本、对注射的恐惧和不完全剂量限制了吸收。所声称的可接受性很高,但并没有始终如一地转化为 疫苗接种,这表明意图与行为之间存在差距,仅靠信息宣传活动不太可能弥合。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解赞比亚农村地区家长对 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 的看法和接受度,鉴于 疫苗 在预防 宫颈癌(赞比亚妇女癌症死亡的主要原因)方面的重要性,解决此类环境中家长如何理解和决定 疫苗 的知识差距。
设计与对象
赞比亚农村 Kabompo 医院附属健康中心进行了一项描述性现象学研究,涉及 20 名 9-14 岁女孩的父母和法定监护人,采用最大变异抽样方法进行选择。
核心方法
该研究涉及用英语或卢瓦尔语进行的录音深度访谈,逐字转录,并使用 Colaizzi 的七步法和 QDA Miner Lite 进行分析。
主要结果
该研究确定了五个主题:对 HPV 及其 疫苗 的知识和认识、对安全性和有效性的看法、社会文化和宗教影响、卫生工作者的作用以及获取障碍。大多数家长都知道 疫苗 可以预防 宫颈癌,但对 HPV 如何导致癌症或 疫苗接种 时间表了解有限。
解释与创新
该研究强调,所表述的可接受性很高,但并没有始终如一地转化为 疫苗接种,这表明意图与行为之间的差距,仅靠信息宣传不太可能弥合,而且宗教和文化因素对决策有重大影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以通过解决知识差距和文化障碍,为提高家长理解和 疫苗 吸收的策略提供参考,特别是在农村地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括样本量小以及它是在单个农村卫生中心进行的,限制了普遍性。证据强度中等。

Parents perceptions and acceptability of the human papillomavirus vaccine in rural Zambia: a descriptive phenomenological study at Kabompo Hospital Affiliated Health Centre

Authors: Sondashi Bombwe, Sebean Mayimbo, Stella Nankamba
Original abstract (English)
Cervical cancer is a leading cause of cancer death among women in Zambia, and vaccination of girls aged 9-14 years against human papillomavirus (HPV) is the principal primary prevention strategy. Because the vaccine is given to minors, uptake depends on parental consent, which is shaped by how parents perceive the vaccine. In Kabompo, a rural, ethnically diverse district of North-Western Province, only 59% of eligible girls had been vaccinated by 2024, well below the global target, yet no qualitative evidence existed on how parents in such settings understand and decide about the vaccine. We conducted a descriptive phenomenological study at Kabompo Hospital Affiliated Health Centre between June and August 2025. Twenty parents and legal guardians of girls aged 9-14 years were purposively selected using maximum variation sampling and interviewed in depth in English or Luvale. Interviews were audio-recorded, transcribed verbatim and analysed using Colaizzi's seven-step method with QDA Miner Lite. Five themes were generated: knowledge and awareness of HPV and its vaccine; perceptions of safety and efficacy; sociocultural and religious influences; the role of health workers; and barriers to access. Most parents knew the vaccine prevents cervical cancer, but few could explain how HPV causes cancer or state the schedule, and several first learned about the vaccine at the point of service. Fear of infertility was the most prominent concern, alongside unease that the vaccine was too new. Religious framings of divine protection, preference for herbal remedies, and concern that vaccination condones early sexual activity shaped decisions. Health workers were the most trusted source of information and were often decisive. Distance, transport costs, fear of injections and incomplete dosing limited uptake. Stated acceptability was high but did not consistently translate into vaccination, indicating an intention-behaviour gap that information campaigns alone are unlikely to close.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand parents' perceptions and acceptability of the human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccine in rural Zambia, addressing a knowledge gap in how parents in such settings understand and decide about the vaccine, given its importance in preventing cervical cancer, a leading cause of cancer death among women in Zambia.
Design and population
A descriptive phenomenological study was conducted at Kabompo Hospital Affiliated Health Centre in rural Zambia, involving 20 parents and legal guardians of girls aged 9-14 years, selected using maximum variation sampling.
Core methods
The study involved audio-recorded, in-depth interviews in English or Luvale, transcribed verbatim, and analyzed using Colaizzi's seven-step method with QDA Miner Lite.
Main results
The study identified five themes: knowledge and awareness of HPV and its vaccine, perceptions of safety and efficacy, sociocultural and religious influences, the role of health workers, and barriers to access. Most parents knew the vaccine prevents cervical cancer but had limited understanding of how HPV causes cancer or the vaccination schedule.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlighted that stated acceptability was high but did not consistently translate into vaccination, indicating an intention-behaviour gap that information campaigns alone are unlikely to close, and that religious and cultural factors significantly influenced decisions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform strategies to improve parental understanding and vaccine uptake, particularly in rural settings, by addressing knowledge gaps and cultural barriers.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include its small sample size and the fact that it was conducted in a single rural health centre, limiting generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.

人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 宫颈筛查自我收集计划的考虑因素:一项定性研究,旨在为印度南部弱势妇女共同设计筛查试验提供信息

Considerations for human papillomavirus (HPV) self-collection programs for cervical screening: a qualitative study to inform co-design of a screening trial among vulnerable women from south India
作者： Anu Mary Oommen, Dhayanidhi Suresh, Maleeha Ashfaq, Tabeetha Malini, P. D, Pavan Kumar Mukherjee, P. P., Pravin Singarayar, Nirmal Ebenezer, John Paul D
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 鉴于低收入和中等收入国家预计计划将向 HPV 筛查过渡,有必要利用 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 自我采集共同设计针对具体情况的宫颈筛查计划。我们进行了一项联合设计研究,计划在印度泰米尔纳德邦的农村、部落和城市贫民窟环境中进行自我收集试验。方法 2023 年,我们在从未接受过筛查的女性中进行了 9 次焦点小组讨论,并采访了 18 名之前使用醋酸目视检查进行过筛查的女性。开放归纳编码之后是使用能力机会动机-行为(COM-B)模型进行演绎框架分析,以识别自我收集的障碍和促进因素。结果 从未接受过筛查的女性报告称,由于无症状、恐惧和尴尬、对癌症的耻辱和恐惧、意识低下以及前往机构接受筛查存在障碍,因此缺乏感知需求。筛选的原因包括社区外展和值得信赖的提供者/信息来源提供的机会。自助取货基本上是可以接受的,被认为是无痛且容易的。女性更喜欢在小组信息会议后在家中或社区自行收集。一些女性对自我效能有怀疑,对疼痛感到恐惧,或者不相信需要进行筛查。在同伴影响是决策重要因素的环境中,团体教育可能有助于激励无症状女性。结论 自助收集,尤其是通过基于群体的社区外展提供的方式,对于资源匮乏地区的大多数妇女来说是可以接受的。需要共同努力来激励女性进行筛查,因为即使自行收集,筛查需求仍然存在竞争性优先事项和低感知需求。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在共同设计使用 HPV 自我采集进行宫颈筛查的具体环境计划,解决低收入和中等收入国家向 HPV 筛查的预期过渡。鉴于现有的知识差距和缺乏针对弱势群体的量身定制的方法,这一背景凸显了此类计划的必要性。
设计与对象
在印度泰米尔纳德邦进行了一项联合设计研究,涉及 9 名从未接受过筛查的女性和 18 名之前使用醋酸目视检查进行过筛查的女性进行的焦点小组讨论。该研究旨在确定农村、部落和城市贫民窟环境中自我收集的障碍和促进因素。
核心方法
该研究采用定性方法,包括焦点小组讨论和访谈,以收集女性对 HPV 自我采集的看法和经历的见解。使用开放式归纳编码和 COM-B 模型的演绎框架分析来分析数据。
主要结果
结果表明,从未接受过筛查的女性报告了一些障碍,例如缺乏感知需求、恐惧和尴尬、耻辱和意识低下。自助取货在很大程度上是可以接受的,并且被认为是无痛且容易的。妇女更喜欢在小组信息会议后在家中或社区自行收集。
解释与创新
作者对研究结果的解释是,自我收集,特别是通过基于群体的社区外展,对于资源匮乏地区的大多数妇女来说是可以接受的。该研究强调了解决感知需求和提供基于社区的教育以激励女性进行筛查的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为未来在资源匮乏地区进行宫颈筛查的 HPV 自我收集计划的设计提供参考,强调基于社区的教育的必要性并解决筛查的障碍。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其定性性质和泰米尔纳德邦的具体背景而受到限制,可能无法推广到其他环境。由于样本量小且关注特定人群,证据强度中等。

Considerations for human papillomavirus (HPV) self-collection programs for cervical screening: a qualitative study to inform co-design of a screening trial among vulnerable women from south India

Authors: Anu Mary Oommen, Dhayanidhi Suresh, Maleeha Ashfaq, Tabeetha Malini, P. D, Pavan Kumar Mukherjee, P. P., Pravin Singarayar, Nirmal Ebenezer, John Paul D
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background There is a need to co-design context specific programs for cervical screening using human papillomavirus (HPV) self-collection, given the anticipated program transition to HPV screening in low- and middle-income countries. We conducted a co-design study to plan a trial of self-collection in Tamil Nadu, India, among rural, tribal and urban slum settings. Methods In 2023 we conducted nine focus group discussions among never screened women and interviewed 18 women previously screened using Visual Inspection with Acetic acid. Open inductive coding was followed by deductive framework analysis using the Capability Opportunity Motivation – Behaviour (COM-B) model to identify barriers and facilitators to self-collection. Results Never screened women reported a lack of perceived need due to being asymptomatic, fear and embarrassment, stigma and fear of cancer, low awareness and barriers to attending facilities for screening. Reasons for screening included opportunities provided by community outreach and by trusted providers/ sources of information. Self-collection was largely acceptable, perceived to be painless and easy. Women preferred self-collection at home or the community, following group-based information sessions. Some women had doubts regarding self-efficacy, fears regarding pain, or were unconvinced of the need for screening. Group education may help motivate asymptomatic women in settings where peer influence is an important factor for decision making. Conclusions Self-collection, especially if provided through group-based community outreach, would be acceptable to most women in low-resource settings. Concerted efforts are required to motivate women for screening as competing priorities and low perceived need for screening will persist even with self-collection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to co-design context-specific programs for cervical screening using HPV self-collection, addressing the anticipated transition to HPV screening in low- and middle-income countries. The background highlights the need for such programs given the existing knowledge gaps and the lack of tailored approaches for vulnerable populations.
Design and population
A co-design study was conducted in Tamil Nadu, India, involving nine focus group discussions with never screened women and 18 women previously screened using Visual Inspection with Acetic acid. The study aimed to identify barriers and facilitators to self-collection among rural, tribal, and urban slum settings.
Core methods
The study involved qualitative methods, including focus group discussions and interviews, to gather insights from women regarding their perceptions and experiences with HPV self-collection. The data were analyzed using open inductive coding and deductive framework analysis with the COM-B model.
Main results
The results indicated that never screened women reported barriers such as a lack of perceived need, fear and embarrassment, stigma, and low awareness. Self-collection was largely acceptable and perceived as painless and easy. Women preferred self-collection at home or in the community following group-based information sessions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings to suggest that self-collection, especially through group-based community outreach, would be acceptable to most women in low-resource settings. The study highlighted the importance of addressing perceived need and providing community-based education to motivate women for screening.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the design of future HPV self-collection programs for cervical screening in low-resource settings, emphasizing the need for community-based education and addressing perceived barriers to screening.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its qualitative nature and the specific context of Tamil Nadu, which may not be generalizable to other settings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and the focus on a specific population.

刚果民主共和国金沙萨艾滋病毒感染者中 患病率、高危型HPV 类型概况以及与 高危型HPV 感染相关的因素

Prevalence, high-risk HPV type profile, and factors associated with high-risk HPV infection among women living with HIV in Kinshasa, Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Julien Neze-Sebakunzi, Anna Maria Doro Altan, Susanna Ceffa, Giovanni Guidotti, Stefano Capparucci, Fausto Ciccacci, Marlène Musikingala, Antoine Nkuba-Ndaye, Jean-Claude Makangara-Cigolo, Jean-Paul Kompany Kisenzele
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
高危 人乳头瘤病毒 (Hr-HPV) 是这种癌症的主要病原体,但金沙萨 HIV 感染妇女中 Hr-HPV 感染的 患病率 记录仍然很少。
设计与对象
方法 在金沙萨 DREAM 中心接受抗逆转录病毒治疗的 25-65 岁艾滋病毒感染者女性中进行了一项横断面研究。
核心方法
在逻辑回归分析中,WHO 临床分期 3 和 4、抗逆转录病毒治疗持续时间少于 2 年以及 HIV 病毒载量大于或等于 1,000 拷贝/ml 显示与 Hr-HPV 感染具有统计学显着相关性,调整后的 OR 为 1.75 (1.16–2.64;p = 0.008)、1.91 (1.07–3.41;p = 0.028)、和 3.13 (1.30–7.54;p = 0.011)。
主要结果
非 16/18 HPV 类型占主导地位,而大约四分之一的 Hr-HPV 阳性女性中检测到 HPV-16 和 HPV-18。
解释与创新
结论 这项研究揭示了金沙萨 HIV 感染女性中 Hr-HPV 感染率较高。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了更广泛的 HPV 疫苗 覆盖范围(包括高价 疫苗)以及加强 宫颈癌 筛查作为综合 宫颈癌 预防策略的一部分的潜在价值。
局限与证据强度
晚期 HIV 疾病(WHO 临床 3 期和 4 期)、未抑制的 HIV 病毒载量以及较短的抗逆转录病毒治疗持续时间与 Hr-HPV 感染独立相关。

Prevalence, high-risk HPV type profile, and factors associated with high-risk HPV infection among women living with HIV in Kinshasa, Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Julien Neze-Sebakunzi, Anna Maria Doro Altan, Susanna Ceffa, Giovanni Guidotti, Stefano Capparucci, Fausto Ciccacci, Marlène Musikingala, Antoine Nkuba-Ndaye, Jean-Claude Makangara-Cigolo, Jean-Paul Kompany Kisenzele
Original abstract (English)
Background Cervical cancer is one of the most common cancers in women, particularly among women living with HIV. High-risk human papillomavirus (Hr-HPV) is the main causative agent of this cancer, but the prevalence of Hr-HPV infection among women living with HIV in Kinshasa remains poorly documented. The objective of this study was to determine this prevalence and identify associated risk factors. Methods A cross-sectional study was conducted among women living with HIV aged 25–65 years receiving antiretroviral therapy at the DREAM Center in Kinshasa. Participants were consecutively recruited among eligible women attending the center during the study period. Cervical samples were collected using the Abbott Cervi–Collect kit and analyzed for Hr-HPV genotyping using the Abbott RealTime HPV assay on the m2000 platform, a real-time PCR method detecting HPV-16, HPV-18, and a pooled group of 12 additional high-risk HPV types with an internal β-globin control for sample quality assurance. Sociodemographic and clinical data were collected using structured questionnaires. Results A total of 436 women were included in the analysis. The prevalence of Hr-HPV infection was 47.7%. Of these, 23.79 % were infected with oncogenic HPV-16 and−18 types (alone or in co-infection with other types). In a logistic regression analysis, WHO clinical stage 3 and 4, duration of antiretroviral therapy for less than 2 years, and HIV viral load greater than or equal to 1,000 copies/ml showed statistically significant associations with Hr-HPV infection, with adjusted ORs of 1.75 (1.16–2.64; p = 0.008), 1.91 (1.07–3.41; p = 0.028), and 3.13 (1.30–7.54; p = 0.011), respectively. Conclusions This study reveals a high prevalence of Hr-HPV infection among women living with HIV in Kinshasa. Non-16/18 HPV types were predominant, while HPV-16 and HPV-18 were detected in approximately one in four Hr-HPV-positive women. Advanced HIV disease (WHO clinical stages 3 and 4), unsuppressed HIV viral load, and shorter duration of antiretroviral therapy were independently associated with Hr-HPV infection. These findings underscore the potential value of broader HPV vaccine coverage, including higher-valent vaccines, alongside strengthened cervical cancer screening as part of comprehensive cervical cancer prevention strategies.

Question and background
High-risk human papillomavirus (Hr-HPV) is the main causative agent of this cancer, but the prevalence of Hr-HPV infection among women living with HIV in Kinshasa remains poorly documented.
Design and population
Methods A cross-sectional study was conducted among women living with HIV aged 25–65 years receiving antiretroviral therapy at the DREAM Center in Kinshasa.
Core methods
In a logistic regression analysis, WHO clinical stage 3 and 4, duration of antiretroviral therapy for less than 2 years, and HIV viral load greater than or equal to 1,000 copies/ml showed statistically significant associations with Hr-HPV infection, with adjusted ORs of 1.75 (1.16–2.64; p = 0.008), 1.91 (1.07–3.41; p = 0.028), and 3.13 (1.30–7.54; p = 0.011), respectively.
Main results
Non-16/18 HPV types were predominant, while HPV-16 and HPV-18 were detected in approximately one in four Hr-HPV-positive women.
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusions This study reveals a high prevalence of Hr-HPV infection among women living with HIV in Kinshasa.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the potential value of broader HPV vaccine coverage, including higher-valent vaccines, alongside strengthened cervical cancer screening as part of comprehensive cervical cancer prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Advanced HIV disease (WHO clinical stages 3 and 4), unsuppressed HIV viral load, and shorter duration of antiretroviral therapy were independently associated with Hr-HPV infection.

凋亡半胱天冬酶在 人乳头瘤病毒 31 型高效复制过程中抑制 MDA5 驱动的 IFN 反应

Apoptotic caspases suppress an MDA5-driven IFN response during productive replication of human papillomavirus type 31
作者： Blossom Damania, Ning Huang, Des’ree Groover, Cary A. Moody
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染复层上皮的基底增殖细胞,但生命周期的生产阶段(包括病毒 基因组 扩增、晚期基因表达和病毒粒子组装)仅限于高度分化的基底上细胞。虽然人们对 HPV 用于阻止未分化细胞中先天 免疫应答 激活的机制了解很多,但关于 HPV 如何阻止分化时的干扰素 (IFN) 反应却知之甚少。在这里,我们证明 高危型HPV 劫持了凋亡半胱天冬酶的天然功能,以抑制分化上皮细胞中的 IFN 反应。我们发现,半胱天冬酶抑制会导致 I 型和 III 型干扰素的分泌,这些干扰素可以以旁分泌方式诱导干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 的表达并阻止 HPV31 的有效复制。重要的是,我们证明 IFN 的表达是由黑色素瘤分化相关基因 5 (MDA5)-线粒体 抗病毒药物-信号蛋白 (MAVS)-TBK1(TANK 结合激酶 1)途径触发的,这表明对双链 RNA (dsRNA) 的反应。此外,我们还确定了 MDA5 和 MAVS 在正常 HPV 生命周期中限制有效病毒复制的作用。这项研究确定了 HPV 通过 caspase 激活重新编程分化细胞的细胞环境的机制,选择通常参与细胞凋亡的蛋白质的非死亡功能来阻断 抗病毒药物 信号传导并促进病毒复制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 如何在分化过程中预防干扰素 (IFN) 反应,因为尽管生命周期的这一阶段在病毒复制中很重要,但人们对其了解较少。
设计与对象
该研究重点关注高风险 人乳头瘤病毒 31 型 (HPV31),并检查其与分化上皮细胞的相互作用,特别关注生产性复制过程中 IFN 反应的抑制。
核心方法
研究人员证明,HPV31会劫持凋亡的caspase来抑制IFN反应,利用实验方法表明caspase抑制会导致IFN分泌以及随后的抗病毒药物效应。
主要结果
研究发现,半胱天冬酶抑制会导致 I 型和 III 型干扰素的分泌,这些干扰素以旁分泌方式诱导干扰素刺激基因 (ISG) 并阻止 HPV31 的有效复制。
解释与创新
作者将他们的发现解释为证据,表明 HPV31 通过 caspase 激活重新编程分化细胞的细胞环境,选择凋亡蛋白的非死亡功能来阻断 抗病毒药物 信号传导并促进病毒复制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了对 HPV31 在分化过程中逃避 IFN 反应的机制的深入了解,这可以为 HPV 相关疾病的 监测、诊断 和 治疗 策略提供参考。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅基于抽象证据,虽然它确定了一种新颖的机制,但缺乏详细的实验数据限制了结论的可信度。

Apoptotic caspases suppress an MDA5-driven IFN response during productive replication of human papillomavirus type 31

Authors: Blossom Damania, Ning Huang, Des’ree Groover, Cary A. Moody
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomaviruses (HPVs) infect the basal proliferating cells of the stratified epithelium, but the productive phase of the life cycle (consisting of viral genome amplification, late gene expression, and virion assembly) is restricted to the highly differentiated suprabasal cells. While much is known regarding the mechanisms that HPVs use to block activation of an innate immune response in undifferentiated cells, little is known concerning how HPV prevents an interferon (IFN) response upon differentiation. Here, we demonstrate that high-risk HPVs hijack a natural function of apoptotic caspases to suppress an IFN response in differentiating epithelial cells. We show that caspase inhibition results in the secretion of type I and type III IFNs that can act in a paracrine manner to induce expression of interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs) and block productive replication of HPV31. Importantly, we demonstrate that the expression of IFNs is triggered by the melanoma differentiation-associated gene 5 (MDA5)-mitochondrial antiviral-signaling protein (MAVS)-TBK1 (TANK-binding kinase 1) pathway, signifying a response to double-stranded RNA (dsRNA). Additionally, we identify a role for MDA5 and MAVS in restricting productive viral replication during the normal HPV life cycle. This study identifies a mechanism by which HPV reprograms the cellular environment of differentiating cells through caspase activation, co-opting a nondeath function of proteins normally involved in apoptosis to block antiviral signaling and promote viral replication.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how human papillomaviruses (HPVs) prevent an interferon (IFN) response during differentiation, as this phase of the life cycle is less understood despite its importance in viral replication.
Design and population
The study focused on high-risk human papillomavirus type 31 (HPV31) and examined its interaction with differentiating epithelial cells, specifically looking at the suppression of IFN responses during productive replication.
Core methods
The researchers demonstrated that HPV31 hijacks apoptotic caspases to suppress IFN responses, using experimental methods to show that caspase inhibition leads to IFN secretion and subsequent antiviral effects.
Main results
The study found that caspase inhibition results in the secretion of type I and type III IFNs, which act in a paracrine manner to induce interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs) and block productive replication of HPV31.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted their findings as evidence that HPV31 reprograms the cellular environment of differentiating cells through caspase activation, co-opting a non-death function of apoptotic proteins to block antiviral signaling and promote viral replication.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the mechanisms by which HPV31 evades the IFN response during differentiation, which could inform strategies for surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of HPV-associated diseases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on abstract-only evidence, and while it identifies a novel mechanism, the lack of detailed experimental data limits the confidence in the conclusions.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

由卵巢成熟囊性畸胎瘤引起的鳞状细胞癌,并发宫颈 高危型HPV 感染和低度鳞状上皮内病变:病例报告

Squamous cell carcinoma arising from ovarian mature cystic teratoma with concurrent cervical high-risk HPV infection and low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion: a case report
作者： Wenna Zhao, Yaxuan Sun, Masmuha Marshed Al Ramadhany, Ruochen Wu, Yifang Zhang
摘要中文翻译结论对于卵巢畸胎瘤患者,当患者年龄较大、肿块较大、肿瘤标志物升高时,需警惕其恶变。资助 作者表示,这项研究没有外部资助。摘要 目的卵巢成熟囊性畸胎瘤(MCT)恶变并不常见,其中以鳞状细胞癌(SCC)为主。人们对其 发病机制 的了解仍知之甚少,而且 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染的潜在作用也存在争议。病例报告 一名 58 岁绝经后妇女因盆腔巨大复杂囊性实性肿块就诊。血清SCC抗原升高揭示了潜在的恶性转化。术中冰冻切片显示为成熟囊性畸胎瘤衍生的鳞状细胞癌,并进行了综合分期手术。组织病理学检查证实国际妇产科联合会(FIGO)IA期SCC由MCT引起。患者经过手术和四个周期的卡铂/紫杉醇辅助化疗后获得完全缓解,24个月随访时未出现复发。值得注意的是,该病例被发现有HPV感染和低度鳞状上皮内病变。宫颈病变HPV阳性,P16表达,而卵巢肿瘤HPV阴性,P16阴性,P53阳性。但由于缺乏基于基因组测序的实质性证据,尚不清楚本案的发病机制中是否存在基因突变驱动机制的作用。其他人正在查看类似内容 缩写 - MCT: - 成熟囊性畸胎瘤 - SCC: - 鳞状细胞癌 - SCC-Ag: - SCC 抗原 - SCC-MCT: - MCT 中出现的 SCC - MRI:...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探讨人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)感染在卵巢成熟囊性畸胎瘤(MCT)恶性转化中的潜在作用,因为对其发病机制仍知之甚少,并且HPV的作用存在争议。
设计与对象
这是一名 58 岁绝经后妇女的 病例报告,患有巨大的复杂囊性实性盆腔肿块,被发现患有 MCT 引起的鳞状细胞癌 (SCC),并发宫颈 高危型HPV 感染和低度鳞状上皮内病变。
核心方法
该研究涉及术中冰冻切片分析、组织病理学检查以及血清 SCC 抗原等肿瘤生物标志物的评估。然而,并未进行基因组测序来确定基因 突变 驱动机制在本例 发病机制 中的作用。
主要结果
宫颈病变HPV阳性,P16表达,而卵巢肿瘤HPV阴性,P16阴性,P53阳性。患者经过手术和四个周期的卡铂/紫杉醇辅助化疗后获得完全缓解,24个月随访时未出现复发。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为强调需要对患有大型卵巢畸胎瘤和肿瘤生物标志物升高的老年患者的恶性转化保持警惕。该病例还表明,在这种情况下,HPV 感染与低度鳞状上皮内病变之间可能存在关联。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提醒临床医生考虑患有大型卵巢畸胎瘤和肿瘤生物标志物升高的老年患者的恶性转化,并强调在此类病例中评估 HPV 状态对 诊断 和 治疗 潜在影响的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏实质性基因组测序证据来阐明基因 突变 驱动机制在 发病机制 病例中的作用,该研究受到限制。该证据基于单个 病例报告,这限制了普遍性。

Squamous cell carcinoma arising from ovarian mature cystic teratoma with concurrent cervical high-risk HPV infection and low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion: a case report

Authors: Wenna Zhao, Yaxuan Sun, Masmuha Marshed Al Ramadhany, Ruochen Wu, Yifang Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Conclusion For patients with ovarian teratoma, it is necessary to be vigilant about the malignant transformation when the patients are elderly with a large mass and elevated tumor biomarkers. Funding The authors indicated that there was no external funding for this study. Abstract Objective Malignant transformation of ovarian mature cystic teratoma (MCT) is uncommon, with squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) being the predominant type. Its pathogenesis remains poorly understood, and the potential role of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection is controversial. Case report A 58-year-old postmenopausal woman presented with a huge complex cystic-solid pelvic mass. The elevated Serum SCC antigen revealed potential malignant transformation. Intraoperative frozen section revealed squamous cell carcinoma arising from mature cystic teratoma, and comprehensive staging surgery was performed. Histopathological examination confirmed the International Federation of Gynecology and Obstetrics (FIGO) stage IA SCC arising from MCT. The patient achieved complete remission after surgery and four cycles of adjuvant carboplatin/paclitaxel chemotherapy, with no recurrence at 24-month follow-up. Notably, this case was found to have the HPV infection and a low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion. The cervical lesion was HPV positive with P16 expression, while the ovarian tumor demonstrated HPV negative with P16 negativity and P53 positivity. However, it is not clear whether the role of the gene mutation-driven mechanism is in the pathogenesis of this case, since substantive evidence based on genomic sequencing is lacking. Similar content being viewed by others Abbreviations - MCT: - Mature cystic teratoma - SCC: - Squamous cell carcinoma - SCC-Ag: - SCC antigen - SCC-MCT: - SCC arising in MCT - MRI:...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the potential role of human papillomavirus (HPV) infection in the malignant transformation of ovarian mature cystic teratoma (MCT), as its pathogenesis remains poorly understood and the role of HPV is controversial.
Design and population
This was a case report of a 58-year-old postmenopausal woman with a huge complex cystic-solid pelvic mass, who was found to have squamous cell carcinoma (SCC) arising from MCT and concurrent cervical high-risk HPV infection and low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion.
Core methods
The study involved intraoperative frozen section analysis, histopathological examination, and assessment of tumor biomarkers such as serum SCC antigen. However, genomic sequencing was not performed to determine the role of gene mutation-driven mechanisms in the pathogenesis of this case.
Main results
The cervical lesion was HPV positive with P16 expression, while the ovarian tumor demonstrated HPV negativity with P16 negativity and P53 positivity. The patient achieved complete remission after surgery and four cycles of adjuvant carboplatin/paclitaxel chemotherapy, with no recurrence at 24-month follow-up.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as highlighting the need for vigilance regarding malignant transformation in elderly patients with large ovarian teratomas and elevated tumor biomarkers. The case also suggests a possible association between HPV infection and low-grade squamous intraepithelial lesion in this context.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution clinicians to consider malignant transformation in elderly patients with large ovarian teratomas and elevated tumor biomarkers, and emphasize the importance of evaluating HPV status in such cases for potential implications in diagnosis and treatment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the lack of substantive genomic sequencing evidence to clarify the role of gene mutation-driven mechanisms in the pathogenesis of the case. The evidence is based on a single case report, which limits generalizability.

L2表位呈现病毒样颗粒作为针对皮肤属β 人乳头瘤病毒的广谱疫苗候选者

L2 epitope presenting virus-like particles as broad-spectrum vaccine candidates targeting cutaneous genus beta human papillomaviruses
作者： Huber Bettina, Schüchner Stefan, Shafti-Keramat Saeed, Mohr Katharina, Kirnbauer Reinhard, Ogris Egon, Bettina Huber, Reinhard Kirnbauer, Egon Ogris
摘要中文翻译摘要 获得许可的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 不针对与免疫抑制个体皮肤癌发展有关的皮肤属 β (ß)HPV 类型。次要衣壳蛋白的保守 N 末端为开发广谱 疫苗 候选药物提供了机会。我们采用了序贯免疫策略,使用五种 ßHPV 的 N 末端 L2 片段,诱导针对不同 ßHPV 类型的广泛体液免疫。产生了四种单克隆抗体 (mAb),可交叉中和多种 ßHPV 类型,并针对潜在致癌 HPV5/24/38 提供体内保护。MAb 表位被鉴定并插入 HPV16 L1 病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的 DE 表面环中。使用嵌合 VLP 进行免疫可诱导针对多种 ßHPV 类型的交叉中和抗体,并赋予体内针对 HPV5/38/76 感染的保护作用。显示 βHPV L2 交叉中和表位的嵌合 VLP 代表了有前景的广谱 疫苗 候选药物,可对抗 βHPV 感染以及免疫抑制患者中角化细胞皮肤癌发生的相关风险。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
获得许可的 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗 不针对与免疫抑制个体皮肤癌发展有关的皮肤属 β (ß)HPV 类型。该研究旨在通过开发针对这些特定 HPV 类型的广谱 疫苗 候选药物来弥补这一差距。
设计与对象
该研究采用了使用五种 ßHPV 的 N 末端 L2 片段的序贯免疫策略。它涉及生成单克隆抗体 (mAb) 并在体内测试其针对多种 ßHPV 类型的交叉中和作用。
核心方法
研究人员产生了四种单克隆抗体 (mAb),可以交叉中和多种 ßHPV 类型。他们将已识别的 MAb 表位插入 HPV16 L1 病毒样颗粒 (VLP) 的 DE 表面环中,以创建用于免疫的嵌合 VLP。
主要结果
使用嵌合 VLP 进行免疫可诱导针对多种 ßHPV 类型的交叉中和抗体。嵌合VLP赋予体内针对HPV5/38/76感染的保护作用。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,展示 βHPV L2 交叉中和表位的嵌合 VLP 可以诱导针对 βHPV 类型的广谱免疫。该方法代表了针对 βHPV 感染的 疫苗 开发的新策略。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,这些嵌合 VLP 可用于开发 疫苗,以预防 βHPV 感染,而βHPV 感染与免疫抑制患者发生角质形成细胞皮肤癌的风险相关。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于针对特定 βHPV 类型的体内保护,并且由于研究结果仅为抽象性质,证据强度中等。

L2 epitope presenting virus-like particles as broad-spectrum vaccine candidates targeting cutaneous genus beta human papillomaviruses

Authors: Huber Bettina, Schüchner Stefan, Shafti-Keramat Saeed, Mohr Katharina, Kirnbauer Reinhard, Ogris Egon, Bettina Huber, Reinhard Kirnbauer, Egon Ogris
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Licensed human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines do not target cutaneous genus beta (ß)HPV types implicated in skin cancer development in immunosuppressed individuals. The conserved N-terminus of minor capsid protein offers the opportunity to develop broad-spectrum vaccine candidates. We employed a sequential immunization strategy using N-terminal L2 fragments of five ßHPVs that induced a broad humoral immunity against different ßHPV types. Four monoclonal antibodies (mAb) were generated that cross-neutralized multiple ßHPV types and conferred in vivo protection against potentially oncogenic HPV5/24/38. MAb epitopes were identified and inserted into the DE surface-loop of HPV16 L1 virus-like particles (VLP). Immunizations with chimeric VLPs induced cross-neutralizing antibodies against multiple ßHPV types and conferred in vivo protection against HPV5/38/76 infection. Chimeric VLPs displaying βHPV L2 cross-neutralizing epitopes represent promising broad-spectrum vaccine candidates against βHPV infection and the associated risk of keratinocytic skin cancerogenesis in immunosuppressed patients.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Licensed human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccines do not target cutaneous genus beta (ß)HPV types implicated in skin cancer development in immunosuppressed individuals. The study aimed to address this gap by developing broad-spectrum vaccine candidates against these specific HPV types.
Design and population
The study employed a sequential immunization strategy using N-terminal L2 fragments of five ßHPVs. It involved generating monoclonal antibodies (mAb) and testing their cross-neutralizing effects against multiple ßHPV types in vivo.
Core methods
The researchers generated four monoclonal antibodies (mAb) that cross-neutralized multiple ßHPV types. They inserted identified MAb epitopes into the DE surface-loop of HPV16 L1 virus-like particles (VLP) to create chimeric VLPs for immunization.
Main results
Immunizations with chimeric VLPs induced cross-neutralizing antibodies against multiple ßHPV types. The chimeric VLPs conferred in vivo protection against HPV5/38/76 infection.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that chimeric VLPs displaying βHPV L2 cross-neutralizing epitopes can induce broad-spectrum immunity against βHPV types. This approach represents a novel strategy for vaccine development targeting βHPV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that these chimeric VLPs could be used to develop vaccines that protect against βHPV infection, which is associated with the risk of keratinocytic skin cancerogenesis in immunosuppressed patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo protection against specific βHPV types, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the abstract-only nature of the findings.

人乳头瘤病毒与乳腺癌的因果关系

Human papilloma virus as causal in breast cancer
作者： Lawson J, Glenn W
摘要中文翻译摘要:本综述的目的是探讨高危型 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在乳腺癌中的作用。在 43 项病例对照研究中的 38 项 (88%) 中,HPV 阳性乳腺癌病例与对照之间的差异具有统计学意义。一项大型前瞻性 队列研究 研究表明,感染 HPV 的女性患乳腺癌的可能性是年龄匹配的未感染女性的 1.4 倍 (11)。虽然这一发现证实了时间标准,但仍需要前瞻性 队列研究 研究。暴露和传播 HPV 主要通过性传播。乳腺癌中的因果关系或非因果关系最重要的证据包括(i)乳腺癌中高危致癌HPV的识别,(ii)在43项病例对照研究中,88%的HPV阳性乳腺癌病例和对照之间的差异具有统计学意义,(iii)在唯一的前瞻性队列研究研究中,感染HPV的女性患乳腺癌的可能性是未感染HPV的同龄女性的1.4倍,以及(iv) HPV 疫苗接种 降低年轻女性乳腺癌的 发病率。总体考虑,证据符合 A。聚合酶链式反应 (PCR) 是最常用的检测方法。HPV 已确定与子宫颈癌、肛门癌、阴茎癌、阴道癌、外阴癌和口咽癌有关。高危型HPV 已在 23 个国家的乳腺癌中持续检测到。在 32.7% 的乳腺癌中发现了 HPV,而在良性和正常乳腺对照中这一比例为 7.6%。HPV 阳性的良性乳腺病变可进展为 HPV 阳性的恶性肿瘤,这表明 HPV 在癌变中发挥着早期作用。HPV生物活性已通过HPV转录物、HPV致癌蛋白表达(HPV...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 在乳腺癌中的作用,弥补有关 HPV 与这种疾病的潜在因果关系的知识差距。其基本原理是基于乳腺癌中 HPV 的鉴定以及已知 HPV 与其他癌症(例如宫颈癌和肛门癌)的关联。
设计与对象
采用病例对照研究设计,涉及来自多个国家的 3,650 例乳腺癌病例和 1,915 例正常和良性乳腺组织对照。该研究历时数年,包括多种 HPV 检测实验室方法,如 PCR、原位 PCR、新一代测序和免疫组织化学。
核心方法
HPV 检测方法包括 PCR、原位 PCR、新一代测序和病毒癌蛋白的免疫组织化学。应用 Bradford Hill 因果标准,并对病例对照研究进行荟萃分析,以评估乳腺癌中 HPV 的 患病率。
主要结果
在 32.7% 的乳腺癌中发现了 HPV,而在正常和良性乳腺对照中这一比例为 7.6%。HPV16 和 HPV18 是最常见的类型,87% 的研究报告了 HPV33,而 HPV33 主要在亚洲人群中观察到。HPV 阳性乳腺癌的 患病率 为 32.7%,而正常和良性乳腺组织对照的 患病率 为 7.6%。
解释与创新
该研究提供的证据表明,其他地点的 HPV 感染会增加患 HPV 相关乳腺癌的风险。研究表明,HPV 发病率 可使巴西年轻女性的乳腺癌 发病率 降低 18% 至 19%,这支持了 HPV 与乳腺癌之间的因果关系。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,HPV 感染可能在乳腺癌病因学中发挥作用,这可以为 监测、诊断 和预防策略提供信息。HPV 发病率 可能是减少乳腺癌 发病率 的有前途的方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性包括使用不同的检测方法、潜在的抽样偏差以及需要更大规模的前瞻性研究来证实研究结果。由于该研究的观察性质以及需要进一步研究以确定因果关系,证据强度中等。

Human papilloma virus as causal in breast cancer

Authors: Lawson J, Glenn W
Original abstract (English)
AbstractThe aim of this review is to consider the role of high-risk human papillomaviruses (HPVs) in breast cancer. In 38 (88%) of the 43 case-control studies the difference between HPV-positive breast cancer cases and controls was statistically significant.A large prospective cohort study demonstrated that women with HPV were 1.4 times more likely to develop breast cancer than age-matched uninfected women (11). While this finding provides a confirmation of a temporal criterion, prospective cohort studies are required.Exposure and transmissionHPVs are primarily sexually transmitted. Causal or not causal in breast cancerThe most important evidence includes (i) identity of high-risk oncogenic HPVs in breast cancers, (ii) in 88% of 43 case-control studies, the difference between HPV-positive breast cancer cases and controls is statistically significant, (iii) in the only prospective cohort study, those women with HPV infections were 1.4 times more likely to develop breast cancer than women of the same age without HPV, and (iv) HPV vaccination reduces the incidence of breast cancer in young women.When considered overall, the evidence meets the A. Polymerase chain reaction (PCR) is the most commonly used method of detection. HPVs have established causal roles in cancers of the cervix, anus, penis, vagina, vulva, and oropharyngeal cancers. High-risk HPVs have been consistently detected in breast cancers in 23 countries. HPVs have been identified in 32.7% of breast cancers as compared to 7.6% of benign and normal breast controls. HPV-positive benign breast lesions can progress to HPV-positive malignancies, suggesting an early role of HPVs in carcinogenesis. HPV biological activity has been demonstrated by the detection of HPV transcripts, HPV oncogenic protein expression (HPV...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of human papillomavirus (HPV) in breast cancer, addressing a knowledge gap regarding the potential causality of HPV in this disease. The rationale was based on the identification of HPVs in breast cancers and the known association of HPV with other cancers, such as cervical and anal cancers.
Design and population
A case-control study design was employed, involving 3,650 breast cancer cases and 1,915 normal and benign breast tissue controls from multiple countries. The study spanned several years and included diverse laboratory approaches for HPV detection, such as PCR, in situ PCR, next-generation sequencing, and immunohistochemistry.
Core methods
HPV detection methods included PCR, in situ PCR, next-generation sequencing, and immunohistochemistry for viral oncoproteins. The Bradford Hill causal criteria were applied, and a meta-analysis of case-control studies was conducted to assess the prevalence of HPV in breast cancers.
Main results
HPVs were identified in 32.7% of breast cancers compared to 7.6% in normal and benign breast controls. HPV16 and HPV18 were the most frequently identified types, reported in 87% of studies, while HPV33 was observed mainly among Asian populations. The prevalence of HPV-positive breast cancers was 32.7% compared to 7.6% in normal and benign breast tissue controls.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provided evidence that HPV infections at other locations can increase the risk of HPV-associated breast cancer. HPV vaccination was shown to reduce the incidence of breast cancer by 18% to 19% in young Brazilian women, supporting the causal relationship between HPV and breast cancer.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that HPV infections may play a role in breast cancer etiology, which could inform surveillance, diagnosis, and prevention strategies. HPV vaccination could be a promising approach to reduce the incidence of breast cancer.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of diverse detection methods, potential sampling bias, and the need for larger prospective studies to confirm the findings. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the need for further research to establish causality.

Among the primary lung SCC cases, 31 (27.2%) cases were positive for p16, and 48.4% showed preserved Rb expression and 51.6% showed complete loss of Rb expression. 切除高危头颈鳞状细胞癌患者对并行帕博利珠单抗和放化疗反应的肿瘤和全身免疫相关性。

Tumoral and Systemic Immune Correlates of Response to Concurrent Pembrolizumab and Chemoradiotherapy in Patients with Resected High-Risk Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma.
作者： Lazar Vujanovic, Pedro Torres-Saavedra, Fei Tu, Ravindra Uppaluri, Min Yao, Josephine Chen, Richard C. Jordan, Jessica L Geiger, Srinivas Jujjavarapu, Arnab Chakravarti
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的:病理学高危、人乳头瘤病毒 阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 患者尽管接受顺铂辅助放疗 (CRT),仍频繁复发。由于 CRT 可以上调 PD-1/L1 免疫检查点,因此添加派姆单抗可能会逆转 治疗 诱导的免疫抑制。NRG-HN003 是一项 I 期试验,评估辅助派姆单抗联合 CRT 的安全性和推荐的 II 期方案。在这里,我们报告了无病生存(DFS)的探索性免疫和基因组相关性。患者和方法:34 名患者接受派姆单抗联合 CRT。通过组合阳性评分 (CPS) 量化 PD-L1 表达,并通过基线肿瘤的多光谱成像评估 PD-L1+ 细胞的空间定位。通过全外显子组测序评估破坏性 TP53 突变。通过 Luminex 评估可溶性血清生物标志物,并通过光谱流式细胞术在基线和 治疗 后对循环免疫子集进行分析。结果:PD-L1 CPS ≥20 的患者的 DFS 数值低于 CPS <20 的患者;然而,较高密度的 PD-L1+ 基质细胞与更有利的结果相关。破坏性 TP53 突变 不是显着的负面预后因素。在基线可溶性标记物中,血清精氨酸酶 1 升高与 DFS 较差相关,而 GM-CSF、IL-5 和 nectin-2 水平较高与更有利的结果相关。在循环免疫细胞中,CD8+ 颗粒酶 K+ T 细胞的基线频率较高,以及治疗后 CD8+ CD39+ 和调节性 CD4+ T 细胞的频率较高,与 DFS 的改善相关。效应和中枢记忆 T 细胞群的增加,尤其是在 治疗 后,也显示出与 DFS 的良好关联。结论:这些发现强调了派姆单抗与 CRT 在高危 HNSCC 中的多种潜在免疫相关性,支持在更大规模、动力充足的试验中进行进一步评估和验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在探索接受派姆单抗辅助放化疗的病理高危、人乳头瘤病毒 阴性头颈鳞状细胞癌 (HNSCC) 患者无病生存的免疫和基因组相关性,解决识别生物标志物以改善患者预后的需要。
设计与对象
一项 I 期试验 (NRG-HN003) 在 34 名高危 HNSCC 患者中评估了辅助派姆单抗联合放化疗的安全性和推荐的 II 期方案。
核心方法
该研究通过多光谱成像结合阳性评分 (CPS) 和 PD-L1+ 细胞的空间定位来评估 PD-L1 表达。通过全外显子组测序评估破坏性 TP53 突变。通过 Luminex 评估可溶性血清生物标志物,并通过光谱流式细胞术在基线和 治疗 后对循环免疫子集进行分析。
主要结果
PD-L1 CPS ≥20 的患者的无病生存期 (DFS) 低于 CPS <20 的患者。PD-L1+ 基质细胞密度越高,预后越好。破坏性 TP53 突变 不是显着的负面预后因素。基线血清精氨酸酶 1 升高与较差的 DFS 相关,而较高水平的 GM-CSF、IL-5 和 nectin-2 与更有利的结果相关。CD8+ 颗粒酶 K+ T 细胞的基线频率较高,以及治疗后 CD8+ CD39+ 和调节性 CD4+ T 细胞的频率较高,与 DFS 的改善相关。效应和中枢记忆 T 细胞群的增加,尤其是在 治疗 后,也显示出与 DFS 的良好关联。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了派姆单抗与高危 HNSCC 放化疗之间的多种潜在免疫相关性,支持在更大规模、动力充足的试验中进行进一步评估和验证。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,特异性免疫和基因组标记物可以预测患者对辅助派姆单抗联合放化疗的反应,这可以为未来的临床试验和针对高危 HNSCC 的个性化 治疗 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到第一阶段设计和小样本量的限制,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。该研究的探索性性质意味着应谨慎解释结果,并在更大规模、动力充足的试验中进行验证。

Tumoral and Systemic Immune Correlates of Response to Concurrent Pembrolizumab and Chemoradiotherapy in Patients with Resected High-Risk Head and Neck Squamous Cell Carcinoma.

Authors: Lazar Vujanovic, Pedro Torres-Saavedra, Fei Tu, Ravindra Uppaluri, Min Yao, Josephine Chen, Richard C. Jordan, Jessica L Geiger, Srinivas Jujjavarapu, Arnab Chakravarti
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Purpose: Patients with pathologically high-risk, human papillomavirus–negative squamous cell carcinoma of the head and neck (HNSCC) recur frequently despite adjuvant cisplatin radiotherapy (CRT). As CRT can upregulate PD-1/L1 immune checkpoints, adding pembrolizumab may reverse treatment-induced immunosuppression. NRG-HN003 was a phase I trial assessing the safety and recommended phase II schedule of adjuvant pembrolizumab with CRT. Here, we report exploratory immune and genomic correlates of disease-free survival (DFS). Patients and Methods: Thirty-four patients received pembrolizumab with CRT. PD-L1 expression was quantified by combined positive score (CPS), and spatial localization of PD-L1+ cells was assessed by multispectral imaging of baseline tumors. Disruptive TP53 mutations were evaluated by whole-exome sequencing. Soluble serum biomarkers were evaluated by Luminex, and circulating immune subsets were profiled by spectral flow cytometry at baseline and after treatment. Results: Patients with PD-L1 CPS ≥20 showed numerically lower DFS than those with CPS <20; however, higher densities of PD-L1+ stromal cells were associated with more favorable outcomes. Disruptive TP53 mutations were not a significant negative prognostic factor. Among soluble markers at baseline, elevated serum arginase-1 was associated with inferior DFS, whereas higher levels of GM-CSF, IL-5 and nectin-2 were associated with more favorable outcomes. In circulating immune cells, higher baseline frequencies of CD8+ granzyme K+ T cells, as well as higher posttreatment frequencies of CD8+ CD39+ and regulatory CD4+ T cells, were associated with improved DFS. Increased effector and central memory T cell populations, especially after treatment, also showed favorable associations with DFS. Conclusions: These findings highlight multiple potential immunologic correlates of pembrolizumab with CRT in high-risk HNSCC, supporting further evaluation and validation in larger, adequately powered trials.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to explore immune and genomic correlates of disease-free survival in patients with pathologically high-risk, human papillomavirus–negative squamous cell carcinoma of the head and neck (HNSCC) who received adjuvant pembrolizumab with chemoradiotherapy, addressing the need to identify biomarkers for improved patient outcomes.
Design and population
A phase I trial (NRG-HN003) assessed the safety and recommended phase II schedule of adjuvant pembrolizumab with chemoradiotherapy in 34 patients with high-risk HNSCC.
Core methods
The study evaluated PD-L1 expression by combined positive score (CPS) and spatial localization of PD-L1+ cells by multispectral imaging. Disruptive TP53 mutations were assessed by whole-exome sequencing. Soluble serum biomarkers were evaluated by Luminex, and circulating immune subsets were profiled by spectral flow cytometry at baseline and after treatment.
Main results
Patients with PD-L1 CPS ≥20 showed numerically lower disease-free survival (DFS) than those with CPS <20. Higher densities of PD-L1+ stromal cells were associated with more favorable outcomes. Disruptive TP53 mutations were not a significant negative prognostic factor. Elevated baseline serum arginase-1 was associated with inferior DFS, while higher levels of GM-CSF, IL-5, and nectin-2 were associated with more favorable outcomes. Higher baseline frequencies of CD8+ granzyme K+ T cells, as well as higher posttreatment frequencies of CD8+ CD39+ and regulatory CD4+ T cells, were associated with improved DFS. Increased effector and central memory T cell populations, especially after treatment, also showed favorable associations with DFS.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight multiple potential immunologic correlates of pembrolizumab with chemoradiotherapy in high-risk HNSCC, supporting further evaluation and validation in larger, adequately powered trials.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that specific immune and genomic markers may predict patient response to adjuvant pembrolizumab with chemoradiotherapy, which could inform future clinical trials and personalized treatment strategies for high-risk HNSCC.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its phase I design and small sample size, which may affect the generalizability of the findings. The exploratory nature of the study means that the results should be interpreted with caution and validated in larger, adequately powered trials.

1996-2022 年哥斯达黎加肛门生殖器癌症 发病率 发病率趋势。

Trends in Incidence Rates of Anogenital Cancers in Costa Rica, 1996-2022.
作者： Loretto J. Carvajal, Jaimie Z. Shing, Juan Carlos Vanegas, Rebeca Ocampo, Emmanuel González, Diego Guillén, Carolina Porras, Rolando Herrero, Alejandro Calderón, Meredith S. Shiels
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估哥斯达黎加 (CR) 年龄标准化 发病率 率 (ASIR) 和侵袭性肛门生殖器癌症 (CR) 的趋势,以解决该地区缺乏此类数据的问题。HPV 是 宫颈癌 的必要原因,约 90% 的肛门癌、78% 的阴道癌、50% 的阴茎癌和 25% 的外阴癌由 HPV 引起。
设计与对象
该研究纳入了 1996 年至 2022 年间的 10,022 例侵袭性肛门生殖器癌症病例,其中 8122 例(81.0%)为 宫颈癌。发病率 数据来自 CR 国家肿瘤登记处,人口估计来自国家统计和普查局。
核心方法
使用 SEER*Stat 计算 ASIR,并使用 Joinpoint 回归分析趋势和年度百分比变化 (APC)。
主要结果
Cervical cancer incidence declined significantly (APC: -3.2%, 95% confidence interval [CI]: -3.9, -2.5). Vaginal cancer showed a non-statistically significant increase (APC: 0.8%, 95% CI: -1.6, 4.1), while vulvar and penile cancers presented a non-statistically significant decrease (APC: -0.8, 95% CI: -1.9, 0.4, and -0.8, 95% CI: -2.1, 1.0, respectively). Anal cancer incidence remained stable (APC: 0.2, 95% CI: -0.9, 1.8) and was 1.4 times higher in females than males (5.3 and 3.7 per million, respectively).
解释与创新
研究发现,宫颈癌 仍然是 CR 中的一个公共卫生问题,需要加强预防。需要对 疫苗 涵盖的 HPV 基因型和持续的肛门生殖器癌症 监测 进行研究,以评估预防效果。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,宫颈癌 发病率 正在下降,但其他肛门生殖器癌症则表现出不同的趋势。为了了解 HPV 监测 和其他预防策略的影响,需要继续进行 监测 和研究。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏 HPV 基因型的详细信息以及可能存在漏报的情况,该研究受到限制。由于该研究的观察性质且缺乏随机对照试验,证据强度中等。

Trends in Incidence Rates of Anogenital Cancers in Costa Rica, 1996-2022.

Authors: Loretto J. Carvajal, Jaimie Z. Shing, Juan Carlos Vanegas, Rebeca Ocampo, Emmanuel González, Diego Guillén, Carolina Porras, Rolando Herrero, Alejandro Calderón, Meredith S. Shiels
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the necessary cause of cervical cancer and responsible for ~90% of anal, 78% of vaginal, 50% of penile, and 25% of vulvar cancers. In Costa Rica (CR), few studies have examined incidence trends of these cancers. We assessed age-standardized incidence rates (ASIRs) and trends of invasive anogenital cancers. Incidence data were obtained from the CR National Registry of Tumors and population estimates from the National Institute of Statistics and Census. ASIRs (1960-Segi World population) were calculated using Surveillance, Epidemiology, and End Results (SEER)*Stat, and trends and annual percent changes (APCs) using Joinpoint regression. During 1996-2022, 10,022 invasive anogenital cancer cases were included in the analysis; of which 8122 (81.0%) were cervical. Cervical cancer incidence declined significantly (APC: -3.2%, 95% confidence interval [CI]: -3.9, -2.5). Vaginal cancer showed a non-statistically significant increase (APC: 0.8%, 95% CI: -1.6, 4.1), while vulvar and penile cancers presented a non-statistically significant decrease (APC: -0.8, 95% CI: -1.9, 0.4, and -0.8, 95% CI: -2.1, 1.0, respectively). Anal cancer incidence remained stable (APC: 0.2, 95% CI: -0.9, 1.8) and was 1.4 times higher in females than males (5.3 and 3.7 per million, respectively). In males, penile cancer incidence was 3-times higher than anal cancer (10.9 vs. Non-cervical anogenital cancers were rare and showed varying trends. Cervical cancer remains a public health concern in CR, requiring stronger prevention. Research on vaccine-covered HPV genotypes and continued surveillance of anogenital cancers are needed to assess prevention impact.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess age-standardized incidence rates (ASIRs) and trends of invasive anogenital cancers in Costa Rica (CR) to address the lack of such data in the region. HPV is the necessary cause of cervical cancer and responsible for ~90% of anal, 78% of vaginal, 50% of penile, and 25% of vulvar cancers.
Design and population
The study included 10,022 invasive anogenital cancer cases from 1996 to 2022, with 8122 (81.0%) being cervical cancers. The incidence data were obtained from the CR National Registry of Tumors and population estimates from the National Institute of Statistics and Census.
Core methods
ASIRs were calculated using SEER*Stat, and trends and annual percent changes (APCs) were analyzed using Joinpoint regression.
Main results
Cervical cancer incidence declined significantly (APC: -3.2%, 95% confidence interval [CI]: -3.9, -2.5). Vaginal cancer showed a non-statistically significant increase (APC: 0.8%, 95% CI: -1.6, 4.1), while vulvar and penile cancers presented a non-statistically significant decrease (APC: -0.8, 95% CI: -1.9, 0.4, and -0.8, 95% CI: -2.1, 1.0, respectively). Anal cancer incidence remained stable (APC: 0.2, 95% CI: -0.9, 1.8) and was 1.4 times higher in females than males (5.3 and 3.7 per million, respectively).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that cervical cancer remains a public health concern in CR, requiring stronger prevention. Research on vaccine-covered HPV genotypes and continued surveillance of anogenital cancers are needed to assess prevention impact.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that cervical cancer incidence is declining, but other anogenital cancers show varying trends. Continued surveillance and research are necessary to understand the impact of HPV vaccination and other prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the lack of detailed information on HPV genotypes and the potential for underreporting. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the absence of randomized controlled trials.

在坦桑尼亚北部乞力马扎罗基督教医疗中心医院接受环形电外科切除手术的女性中,浸润前宫颈病变的复发及相关因素。

Recurrence of preinvasive cervical lesion and associated factors among women who underwent loop electrosurgical excision procedure at Kilimanjaro Christian Medical Centre hospital, northern Tanzania.
作者： Peter Mbinda, Patricia Swai, John Lugata, Nasra Batchu, Tecla Lyamuya, Livin Kanje, Kenneth Bagandanshwa, Eusebious Maro, Bariki Mchome
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
宫颈癌 是一个重大的公共卫生问题。诊断浸润前宫颈病变为早期干预提供了重要机会,以防止进展为 宫颈癌。
设计与对象
这是一项回顾性 队列研究 研究,研究对象包括 2018 年 1 月至 2023 年 3 月期间在 KCMC 进行宫颈浸润前病变筛查的女性,使用醋酸目视检查、巴氏涂片或 人乳头瘤病毒 测试。392 名患有宫颈病变的参与者中,有 196 名接受了 LEEP 治疗并返回进行随访。
核心方法
使用 SPSS 25 版进行数据分析。使用单变量和多变量逻辑回归来检查与浸润前宫颈病变复发相关的因素。
主要结果
Among 7335 women screened for cervical lesions from 2018 to 2023, 392 (5.3%) were positive for cervical lesions, and 286 (3.9%) were suspected of having cervical cancer. The recurrence rate of preinvasive cervical lesions was observed among 16 (8.2%) patients.
解释与创新
在最终的多变量分析中,与 20-34 岁的患者相比,35-49 岁的患者浸润前宫颈病变复发的风险较低(调整后的比值比 [95% 置信区间] = 0.10 [0.02-0.66])。此外,与 HIV 阴性女性相比,HIV 阳性患者的宫颈病变复发风险更高(调整后优势比 [95% 置信区间] = 4.51 [3.89-5.81]),且具有统计学意义,P 值 < 0.005。
科研与公卫意义
监测病毒载量并针对其他高危人群实施量身定制的策略是降低复发风险的关键步骤。报告的 8.2% 的复发率凸显了特殊干预措施的重要性,特别是对于 HIV 阳性女性等高危人群。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告研究设计的局限性或证据强度评估。

Recurrence of preinvasive cervical lesion and associated factors among women who underwent loop electrosurgical excision procedure at Kilimanjaro Christian Medical Centre hospital, northern Tanzania.

Authors: Peter Mbinda, Patricia Swai, John Lugata, Nasra Batchu, Tecla Lyamuya, Livin Kanje, Kenneth Bagandanshwa, Eusebious Maro, Bariki Mchome
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Cervical cancer is a significant public health problem. Diagnosing preinvasive cervical lesion provides an important opportunity in early intervention to prevent progression to cervical cancer. Despite the achievements made in screening and management, the burden of cervical lesions remains high in East Africa, where mortality rates are disproportionately higher. In Tanzania, cervical cancer is the leading cause of death in women, yet there is limited information on the effectiveness of the procedures used in the treatment of preinvasive cervical lesions. OBJECTIVE: This study aims to determine the 1-year recurrence of preinvasive cervical lesions and its associated factors among women who underwent loop electrosurgical excision procedure (LEEP) treatment at Kilimanjaro Christian Medical Centre (KCMC) hospital from January 2018 to March 2023. METHODS: This is a retrospective cohort study including women screened for preinvasive cervical lesions at KCMC between January 2018 and March 2023 using visual inspection with acetic acid, Pap smear, or human papillomavirus testing. SPSS version 25 was used for data analysis. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression were used to check factors associated with the recurrence of preinvasive cervical lesions. RESULTS: Among 7335 women screened for cervical lesions from 2018 to 2023, 392 (5.3%) were positive for cervical lesions, and 286 (3.9%) were suspected of having cervical cancer. From 392 participants with cervical lesions, 196 were treated using LEEP and returned for follow-up. The recurrence rate of preinvasive cervical lesions was observed among 16 (8.2%) patients. In the final multivariable analysis, patients aged 35-49 years had a lower risk of preinvasive cervical lesion recurrence compared to those aged 20-34 years (adjusted odds ratio [95% confidence interval] = 0.10 [0.02-0.66]). Additionally, HIV-positive patients were found to have a higher risk of cervical lesion recurrence compared to HIV-negative women (adjusted odds ratio [95% confidence interval] = 4.51 [3.89-5.81]) and was statistically significant with P-value < 0.005. CONCLUSION: The reported recurrence rate of 8.2% highlights the importance of special interventions, particularly for high-risk groups such as HIV-positive women. Monitoring viral load and implementing tailored strategies for other at-risk demographics are crucial steps to mitigate recurrence risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Cervical cancer is a significant public health problem. Diagnosing preinvasive cervical lesion provides an important opportunity in early intervention to prevent progression to cervical cancer.
Design and population
This is a retrospective cohort study including women screened for preinvasive cervical lesions at KCMC between January 2018 and March 2023 using visual inspection with acetic acid, Pap smear, or human papillomavirus testing. From 392 participants with cervical lesions, 196 were treated using LEEP and returned for follow-up.
Core methods
SPSS version 25 was used for data analysis. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression were used to check factors associated with the recurrence of preinvasive cervical lesions.
Main results
Among 7335 women screened for cervical lesions from 2018 to 2023, 392 (5.3%) were positive for cervical lesions, and 286 (3.9%) were suspected of having cervical cancer. The recurrence rate of preinvasive cervical lesions was observed among 16 (8.2%) patients.
Interpretation and novelty
In the final multivariable analysis, patients aged 35-49 years had a lower risk of preinvasive cervical lesion recurrence compared to those aged 20-34 years (adjusted odds ratio [95% confidence interval] = 0.10 [0.02-0.66]). Additionally, HIV-positive patients were found to have a higher risk of cervical lesion recurrence compared to HIV-negative women (adjusted odds ratio [95% confidence interval] = 4.51 [3.89-5.81]) and was statistically significant with P-value < 0.005.
Scientific and public-health significance
Monitoring viral load and implementing tailored strategies for other at-risk demographics are crucial steps to mitigate recurrence risks. The reported recurrence rate of 8.2% highlights the importance of special interventions, particularly for high-risk groups such as HIV-positive women.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study design or evidence strength assessment.

Journal: Journal of medical Internet research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-15  |  DOI: 10.2196/92040  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 28, e92040
A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

研究人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗相关的社交媒体内容:八种社交媒体平台的范围审查,关注沟通内容、信息来源和分析方法。

Study of Human Papillomavirus Vaccine-Related Social Media Content Across Eight Social Media Platforms: Scoping Review of Communication Content, Information Sources, and Analytical Approaches.
作者： Yinghan Xu, Ruichen Cong, Mingxin Liu, Siyu Zhou, Masahiko Sakaguchi, Kayoko Katayama, Qun Jin, Shoji Nishimura, Atsushi Ogihara
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本范围审查旨在绘制和综合分析HPV 疫苗相关的社交媒体内容的研究,特别关注沟通内容、信息来源和方法学特征。本综述关注人乳头瘤病毒、HPV类型和明确的HPV相关疾病,包括宫颈上皮内瘤变、HPV相关的宫颈癌、HPV相关的肛门癌、HPV相关的口咽癌、生殖器疣和HPV感染。
证据基础与方法
根据范围审查的PRISMA-ScR指南,我们在2026年6月10日搜索了PubMed、Web of Science、MEDLINE、Embase和Scopus。纳入了2006年至2026年间发表的英语原创同行评审研究,这些研究分析了社交媒体上的HPV 疫苗相关的沟通内容。数据使用结构化提取表进行记录,并进行了描述性综合。未进行正式的偏倚风险评估。在2026年7月17日完成了引文追溯和引文追踪。
共识与结论
HPV 疫苗相关的沟通内容被综合为5类:风险和担忧导向的、益处和预防导向的、错误信息和不信任相关的、经验性和叙述性的以及实用性和政策导向的沟通。在研究中,预防和益处信息与安全担忧、副作用、访问障碍、错误信息、不信任和个人叙述共存。个人账户通常与经验性叙述或负面情绪相关,而专业来源较少但通常提供了更具有信息性或高质量的内容。
争议与缺口
证据受到平台、采样策略、数据收集时期、编码方案和分析方法的异质性限制,限制了研究间的直接比较。大多数研究分析了公开可用的社交媒体内容,因此本综述无法确定个体暴露、解释、HPV意图或HPV 疫苗的接受度。本综述还指出,仅通过情感极性无法充分捕捉HPV 疫苗讨论的复杂性。
科研与实践启示
这些发现支持理论导向、平台敏感的研究,并可能指导公共卫生沟通策略,包括基于证据的教育、透明的风险沟通、可信来源的参与、叙述导向的信息传递、错误信息监测和提高信息质量。

Study of Human Papillomavirus Vaccine-Related Social Media Content Across Eight Social Media Platforms: Scoping Review of Communication Content, Information Sources, and Analytical Approaches.

Authors: Yinghan Xu, Ruichen Cong, Mingxin Liu, Siyu Zhou, Masahiko Sakaguchi, Kayoko Katayama, Qun Jin, Shoji Nishimura, Atsushi Ogihara
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Social media has become an important channel for health information dissemination and public discussion of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination. Previous reviews have examined social media and HPV vaccination, often focusing on single platforms, broader HPV-related topics, or the potential effects on knowledge, leaving limited cross-platform synthesis specifically focused on HPV vaccine–related communication content, information sources, and analytical approaches. Objective This scoping review aimed to map and synthesize peer-reviewed studies examining HPV vaccine–related social media content, with particular attention to communication content, information sources, and methodological characteristics. Methods Following the PRISMA-ScR (Preferred Reporting Items for Systematic Reviews and Meta-Analyses extension for Scoping Reviews) guidelines, we searched PubMed, Web of Science, MEDLINE, Embase, and Scopus on June 10, 2026. Backward and forward citation searches were completed on July 17, 2026. We included peer-reviewed English-language original studies published between 2006 and 2026 that analyzed HPV vaccine–related communication content on social media. Data were charted using a structured extraction form and synthesized descriptively. No formal risk-of-bias assessment was conducted. Results A total of 50 studies published between 2012 and 2026 met the eligibility criteria. Eight social media platforms were represented, with X (formerly Twitter; X Corp) most frequently studied, followed by Weibo (Weibo Corporation), and YouTube (Google LLC). HPV vaccine–related communication content was synthesized into 5 categories: risk- and concern-oriented, benefit- and prevention-oriented, misinformation- and distrust-related, experiential and narrative, and practical and policy-related communication. Across studies, prevention and benefit messages coexisted with safety concerns, side effects, access barriers, misinformation, distrust, and personal narratives. Personal accounts were often associated with experiential narratives or negative sentiment, whereas professional sources were less common but generally provided more informative or higher-quality content. Analytical approaches included content analysis, sentiment analysis, topic modeling, and network analysis, but theoretical frameworks were applied in only a minority of studies. Conclusions The evidence was limited by heterogeneity in platforms, sampling strategies, data collection periods, coding schemes, and analytical methods, limiting direct comparison across studies. Because most studies analyzed publicly available social media content, this review could not determine individual-level exposure, interpretation, vaccination intention, or vaccine uptake. Overall, HPV vaccine discourse cannot be adequately understood through sentiment polarity alone. This review provides a cross-platform, content-focused synthesis of how HPV vaccine–related social media communication has been studied. Unlike previous reviews, this review clarifies how HPV vaccine–related content has been described, who generates it, and which methods have been used to analyze it. These findings support theory-informed, platform-sensitive research and may inform public health communication strategies, including evidence-based education, transparent risk communication, credible source engagement, narrative-informed messaging, misinformation monitoring, and improved information quality.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This scoping review aimed to map and synthesize peer-reviewed studies examining HPV vaccine–related social media content, with particular attention to communication content, information sources, and methodological characteristics. The review focused on human papillomaviruses, HPV types, and explicitly HPV-associated diseases, including cervical intraepithelial neoplasia, HPV-associated cervical cancer, HPV-associated anal cancer, HPV-associated oropharyngeal cancer, genital warts, and HPV infection.
Evidence base and method
Following the PRISMA-ScR guidelines, the review searched PubMed, Web of Science, MEDLINE, Embase, and Scopus on June 10, 2026. It included peer-reviewed English-language original studies published between 2006 and 2026 that analyzed HPV vaccine–related communication content on social media. Data were charted using a structured extraction form and synthesized descriptively. No formal risk-of-bias assessment was conducted. Backward and forward citation searches were completed on July 17, 2026.
Consensus and conclusions
HPV vaccine–related communication content was synthesized into 5 categories: risk- and concern-oriented, benefit- and prevention-oriented, misinformation- and distrust-related, experiential and narrative, and practical and policy-related communication. Across studies, prevention and benefit messages coexisted with safety concerns, side effects, access barriers, misinformation, distrust, and personal narratives. Personal accounts were often associated with experiential narratives or negative sentiment, whereas professional sources were less common but generally provided more informative or higher-quality content.
Controversies and gaps
The evidence was limited by heterogeneity in platforms, sampling strategies, data collection periods, coding schemes, and analytical methods, limiting direct comparison across studies. Most studies analyzed publicly available social media content, so this review could not determine individual-level exposure, interpretation, vaccination intention, or vaccine uptake. The review also noted that sentiment polarity alone cannot adequately capture the complexity of HPV vaccine discourse.
Research and practice implications
These findings support theory-informed, platform-sensitive research and may inform public health communication strategies, including evidence-based education, transparent risk communication, credible source engagement, narrative-informed messaging, misinformation monitoring, and improved information quality.

月经血用于人乳头瘤病毒检测在宫颈癌筛查中的诊断准确性:一项系统综述和荟萃分析。

Diagnostic accuracy of menstrual blood for human papillomavirus detection in cervical cancer screening: A systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Prayash Paudel, Asutosh Sah
摘要中文翻译目的:宫颈癌是全球主要的健康负担,尤其在低收入和中等收入国家(LMICs)中对女性影响更大,这些地区传统人乳头瘤病毒(HPV)筛查的可及性有限。月经血(MB)作为一种非侵入性且可被动收集的生物样本,可能提供一种文化上可接受的替代方案。本综述评估了基于月经血的高危型HPV检测与由临床医生收集的宫颈样本相比的诊断准确性。方法:我们纳入了评估自取月经血用于高危型HPV检测的诊断准确性研究和随机对照试验(RCTs),研究对象为无症状的25-65岁女性。符合纳入标准的研究报告了使用临床医生收集的宫颈样本作为参考的敏感性、特异性或2×2数据。我们于2025年7月7日之前在PubMed、Embase和Google Scholar上进行了检索。偏倚风险通过QUADAS-3进行评估,采用双变量随机效应模型估计汇总敏感性和特异性。证据的确定性使用GRADE进行评估。结果:七项研究(n = 1,297名参与者;586名参考阳性,711名参考阴性)符合纳入标准。基于月经血的HPV检测的汇总敏感性和特异性分别为0.86(95% CI:0.80-0.90)和0.90(95% CI:0.72-0.97)。诊断比值比为56(95% CI:19-166),层次SROC曲线下的面积为0.90(95% CI:0.87-0.92),表明总体诊断性能较高。然而,观察到高度异质性(I2 = 94%)和阈值效应。未检测到显著的发表偏倚(Deeks' p = 0.62)。结论:基于月经血的HPV检测显示出高诊断准确性,并成为宫颈癌筛查的一种非侵入性替代方案,特别是在服务不足的地区。需要在多样化的群体中进一步验证。

Diagnostic accuracy of menstrual blood for human papillomavirus detection in cervical cancer screening: A systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Prayash Paudel, Asutosh Sah
Original abstract (English)
PURPOSE: Cervical cancer is a major global health burden, disproportionately affecting women in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), where access to traditional human papillomavirus (HPV) screening is limited. Menstrual blood (MB), a non-invasive and passively collectable biospecimen, may offer a culturally acceptable alternative. This review evaluates the diagnostic accuracy of MB-based high-risk HPV detection compared to clinician-collected cervical samples. METHODS: We included diagnostic accuracy studies and randomized controlled trials (RCTs) evaluating self-collected MB for high-risk HPV detection in asymptomatic women aged 25-65 years. Eligible studies reported sensitivity, specificity, or 2×2 data using clinician-collected cervical samples as the reference. We searched PubMed, Embase, and Google Scholar up to July 7, 2025. Risk of bias was assessed via QUADAS-3, and a bivariate random-effects model estimated pooled sensitivity and specificity. Certainty of evidence was assessed using GRADE. RESULTS: Seven studies (n = 1,297 participants; 586 reference-positive, 711 reference-negative) met inclusion criteria. The pooled sensitivity and specificity of MB-based HPV testing were 0.86 (95% CI: 0.80-0.90) and 0.90 (95% CI: 0.72-0.97), respectively. The diagnostic odds ratio was 56 (95% CI: 19-166), and the area under the hierarchical SROC curve was 0.90 (95% CI: 0.87-0.92), indicating high overall diagnostic performance. However, high heterogeneity (I2 = 94%) and threshold effects were observed. No significant publication bias was detected (Deeks' p = 0.62). CONCLUSIONS: MB-based HPV testing demonstrates high diagnostic accuracy and emerges as a non-invasive alternative for cervical cancer screening, especially in underserved settings. Further validation in diverse populations is warranted.

Menstrual blood (MB), a non-invasive and passively collectable biospecimen, may offer a culturally acceptable alternative.
Evidence base and method
We included diagnostic accuracy studies and randomized controlled trials (RCTs) evaluating self-collected MB for high-risk HPV detection in asymptomatic women aged 25-65 years.
Consensus and conclusions
The pooled sensitivity and specificity of MB-based HPV testing were 0.86 (95% CI: 0.80-0.90) and 0.90 (95% CI: 0.72-0.97), respectively.
Controversies and gaps
However, high heterogeneity (I2 = 94%) and threshold effects were observed.
Research and practice implications
MB-based HPV testing demonstrates high diagnostic accuracy and emerges as a non-invasive alternative for cervical cancer screening, especially in underserved settings.

人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染在致癌过程中的作用以及预防性 疫苗接种 的作用:叙述性回顾

HUMAN PAPILLOMAVIRUS (HPV) INFECTION IN CARCINOGENESIS AND THE ROLE OF PROPHYLACTIC VACCINATION: A NARRATIVE REVIEW
作者： Kinga Bukała, Anton Osnytskyy, Kamil Rutkowski, Magdalena Ślifierska, Daria Furman, Karina Udrycka, Szymon Klimaszewski
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是全世界最流行的性传播病原体之一,也是良性增殖性病变和恶性肿瘤的既定原因。这篇叙述性综述探讨了 HPV 感染在主要解剖部位癌变中的作用,并评估了预防性药物 疫苗接种 对疾病控制的贡献。对 PubMed/MEDLINE、Scopus 和 Web of Science 进行了结构化检索,查找截至 2026 年 8 月可用的英文出版物,并辅以国际卫生当局的最新指南。证据证实高危基因型(主要是 HPV-16 和 HPV-18)在口咽鳞状细胞癌以及子宫颈、外阴、阴道、阴茎和肛门癌症中的因果作用,由 E6 和 E7 癌蛋白通过 p53 和视网膜母细胞瘤途径失活介导。这种关联的强度明显取决于部位:它在口咽、子宫颈和肛门中已得到很好的建立,但在口腔和喉中仍然模棱两可。在口咽癌中,HPV 状态是一个独立的预后因素,并已纳入当前的分期中。疫苗接种 显着减少感染、癌前病变和 宫颈癌 发病率,在首次性行为前免疫的个体中获益最大。疫苗接种 和有组织的筛查是互补而非替代策略,而在波兰观察到的次优吸收仍然是实现现有 疫苗 全面预防潜力的主要障碍。

HUMAN PAPILLOMAVIRUS (HPV) INFECTION IN CARCINOGENESIS AND THE ROLE OF PROPHYLACTIC VACCINATION: A NARRATIVE REVIEW

Authors: Kinga Bukała, Anton Osnytskyy, Kamil Rutkowski, Magdalena Ślifierska, Daria Furman, Karina Udrycka, Szymon Klimaszewski
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is among the most prevalent sexually transmitted pathogens worldwide and an established cause of both benign proliferative lesions and malignant neoplasms. This narrative review examines the role of HPV infection in carcinogenesis at the principal anatomical sites involved and evaluates the contribution of prophylactic vaccination to disease control. A structured search of PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, and Web of Science was conducted for English-language publications available up to August 2026, supplemented by current guidance from international health authorities. The evidence confirms a causal role for high-risk genotypes, principally HPV-16 and HPV-18, in oropharyngeal squamous cell carcinoma and in cancers of the cervix, vulva, vagina, penis, and anus, mediated by the E6 and E7 oncoproteins through inactivation of the p53 and retinoblastoma pathways. The strength of this association is markedly site-dependent: it is well established in the oropharynx, cervix, and anus, but remains equivocal in the oral cavity and larynx. In oropharyngeal carcinoma, HPV status is an independent prognostic factor and is incorporated into current staging. Vaccination substantially reduces infection, precancerous lesions, and cervical cancer incidence, with the greatest benefit among individuals immunized before sexual debut. Vaccination and organized screening are complementary rather than alternative strategies, and suboptimal uptake-as observed in Poland-remains the principal barrier to realizing the full preventive potential of available vaccines.

Scope and question
This narrative review examines the role of HPV infection in carcinogenesis at the principal anatomical sites involved and evaluates the contribution of prophylactic vaccination to disease control.
Evidence base and method
A structured search of PubMed/MEDLINE, Scopus, and Web of Science was conducted for English-language publications available up to August 2026, supplemented by current guidance from international health authorities.
Consensus and conclusions
The strength of this association is markedly site-dependent: it is well established in the oropharynx, cervix, and anus, but remains equivocal in the oral cavity and larynx.
Controversies and gaps
In oropharyngeal carcinoma, HPV status is an independent prognostic factor and is incorporated into current staging.
Research and practice implications
Vaccination and organized screening are complementary rather than alternative strategies, and suboptimal uptake-as observed in Poland-remains the principal barrier to realizing the full preventive potential of available vaccines.

人乳头瘤病毒 疫苗接种作为宫颈癌预防的基础:吉尔吉斯共和国的现状和前景

Human Papillomavirus Vaccination as the Basis for Cervical Cancer Prevention: Current Status and Prospects in the Kyrgyz Republic
作者： A. Amanbekov, Z. Kurmanalieva, Ch.A. Stakeeva, A. Zhakypova, R. Asakeeva
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是导致 宫颈癌 和其他几种肛门生殖器恶性肿瘤发生的主要病因。根据世界卫生组织的数据,超过 95% 的 宫颈癌 病例与 高危型HPV 类型引起的持续感染有关。目前,HPV 疫苗接种被公认为宫颈癌一级预防最有效的方法,并已纳入全球多个国家的国家免疫规划。国际经验表明,接种人群中HPV感染的患病率、高度宫颈上皮内病变和侵袭性宫颈癌显着减少。本研究的目的是分析有关 HPV 疫苗接种 作为 宫颈癌 预防有效策略的现有证据,并评估吉尔吉斯共和国制定免疫计划的前景。对与 HPV 预防和 疫苗接种 相关的国家和国际科学文献、监管文件和官方统计报告进行了审查。对世界卫生组织、国际癌症研究机构和吉尔吉斯共和国卫生部的数据进行了分析。HPV 疫苗接种被发现是预防HPV相关疾病的安全且高效的方法。国际经验分析表明,大规模疫苗接种项目实施后,致癌HPV类型、高级别宫颈病变和宫颈癌 发病率的患病率显着减少。然而,各种组织、社会和信息障碍继续限制包括吉尔吉斯共和国在内的一些国家免疫规划的有效性。扩大 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖范围是 宫颈癌 预防最有前途的策略之一。进一步制定和加强免疫计划可能会大大减少吉尔吉斯共和国女性人群中 HPV 相关疾病的 发病率 和 死亡率。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是导致 宫颈癌 和其他几种肛门生殖器恶性肿瘤发生的主要病因。本研究的目的是分析有关 HPV 疫苗接种 作为 宫颈癌 预防有效策略的现有证据,并评估吉尔吉斯共和国制定免疫计划的前景。
证据基础与方法
对与 HPV 预防和 疫苗接种 相关的国家和国际科学文献、监管文件和官方统计报告进行了审查。对世界卫生组织、国际癌症研究机构和吉尔吉斯共和国卫生部的数据进行了分析。
共识与结论
根据世界卫生组织的数据,超过 95% 的 宫颈癌 病例与 高危型HPV 类型引起的持续感染有关。国际经验表明,接种人群中HPV感染的患病率、高度宫颈上皮内病变和侵袭性宫颈癌显着减少。国际经验分析表明,大规模疫苗接种项目实施后,致癌HPV类型、高级别宫颈病变和宫颈癌 发病率的患病率显着减少。
争议与缺口
然而,各种组织、社会和信息障碍继续限制包括吉尔吉斯共和国在内的一些国家免疫规划的有效性。
科研与实践启示
HPV 疫苗接种被发现是预防HPV相关疾病的安全且高效的方法。扩大 HPV 疫苗接种 覆盖范围是 宫颈癌 预防最有前途的策略之一。进一步制定和加强免疫计划可能会大大减少吉尔吉斯共和国女性人群中 HPV 相关疾病的 发病率 和 死亡率。

Human Papillomavirus Vaccination as the Basis for Cervical Cancer Prevention: Current Status and Prospects in the Kyrgyz Republic

Authors: A. Amanbekov, Z. Kurmanalieva, Ch.A. Stakeeva, A. Zhakypova, R. Asakeeva
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the primary etiological factor responsible for the development of cervical cancer and several other anogenital malignancies. According to the World Health Organization, more than 95% of cervical cancer cases are associated with persistent infection caused by high-risk HPV types. Currently, HPV vaccination is recognized as the most effective method for the primary prevention of cervical cancer and has been incorporated into national immunization programs in many countries worldwide. International experience has demonstrated a substantial reduction in the prevalence of HPV infection, high-grade cervical intraepithelial lesions, and invasive cervical cancer among vaccinated populations. The aim of this study was to analyze current evidence regarding HPV vaccination as an effective strategy for cervical cancer prevention and to assess the prospects for developing immunization programs in the Kyrgyz Republic. A review of national and international scientific literature, regulatory documents, and official statistical reports related to HPV prevention and vaccination was conducted. Data from the World Health Organization, the International Agency for Research on Cancer, and the Ministry of Health of the Kyrgyz Republic were analyzed. HPV vaccination was found to be a safe and highly effective method for preventing HPV-associated diseases. Analysis of international experience demonstrated a significant reduction in the prevalence of oncogenic HPV types, high-grade cervical lesions, and cervical cancer incidence following the implementation of large-scale vaccination programs. However, various organizational, social, and informational barriers continue to limit the effectiveness of immunization programs in a number of countries, including the Kyrgyz Republic. Expanding HPV vaccination coverage represents one of the most promising strategies for cervical cancer prevention. Further development and strengthening of immunization programs may substantially reduce the incidence of and mortality from HPV-associated diseases among the female population of the Kyrgyz Republic.

人乳头瘤病毒 相关癌症的跨 队列研究 转录和网络特征

Cross-cohort transcriptional and network signatures of human papillomavirus-associated cancers
作者： Ebenezer Donkor, Livia Beccacece, Godsway Kwame Awusi, Charles Mills-Robertson, Toufic Suraj, Lily Paemka, Valerio Napolioni
摘要中文翻译简介:持续性 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 感染极大地增加了全球病毒相关癌症的负担,包括宫颈癌、外阴癌、肛门癌、阴茎癌和口咽癌。HPV 阳性肿瘤经常表现出独特的临床特征。将不同组织类型中的 HPV 相关癌症统一起来的分子机制和生物标志物仍知之甚少。材料和方法:检索并统一处理代表三种肿瘤类型的 60 个批量 RNA-seq 数据集。进行荟萃分析以评估肿瘤 队列研究 之间的转录组相似性。进行差异基因表达分析来表征 HPV 阳性和 HPV 阴性表达模式。进行加权基因共表达网络分析(WGCNA)来鉴定 HPV 相关基因模块。使用模块成员资格和基因显着性标准选择中心基因,并通过 队列研究 特异性蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用分析进一步确定优先级。结果:差异表达分析确定了所有三种 队列研究 中与 HPV 状态相关的明显转录差异,尽管差异表达的程度因肿瘤类型而异。交叉 队列研究 荟萃分析确定了一个 2959 个基因的转录程序在固定效应模型下达到了 基因组 范围内的显着性,其中 557 个基因在异质性调整的随机效应模型下仍然具有显着性。基因集富集分析确定了 1578 个达到错误发现率 (FDR) 校正显着性的通路,主要与 免疫应答、DNA 修复和 DNA 复制相关。WGCNA 鉴定了 HPV 相关共表达模块,其中心基因富集用于免疫调节、适应性免疫激活和 抗病毒药物 宿主 防御过程。结论:综合差异表达、异质性感知荟萃分析和共表达网络分析确定了 队列研究 中与 HPV 状态相关的转录模式。这些发现为 HPV 相关癌症的独立 队列研究 的未来验证和功能研究提供了候选基因和生物学途径。
