范围与问题
HPV 阳性肿瘤经常表现出独特的临床特征。
证据基础与方法
进行荟萃分析以评估肿瘤 队列研究 之间的转录组相似性。
共识与结论
差异表达分析确定了所有三种 队列研究 中与 HPV 状态相关的明显转录差异,尽管差异表达的程度因肿瘤类型而异。
争议与缺口
综合差异表达、异质性感知荟萃分析和共表达网络分析确定了 队列研究 中与 HPV 状态相关的转录模式。
科研与实践启示
这些发现为 HPV 相关癌症的独立 队列研究 的未来验证和功能研究提供了候选基因和生物学途径。

Cross-cohort transcriptional and network signatures of human papillomavirus-associated cancers

Authors: Ebenezer Donkor, Livia Beccacece, Godsway Kwame Awusi, Charles Mills-Robertson, Toufic Suraj, Lily Paemka, Valerio Napolioni
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Persistent human papillomavirus (HPV) infection contributes substantially to the global burden of virus-associated cancers, including those of the cervix, vulva, anus, penis, and oropharynx. HPV-positive tumors frequently exhibit distinct clinical characteristics. The molecular mechanisms and biomarkers that unify HPV-associated cancers across various tissue types remain poorly characterized. Materials and methods: Sixty bulk RNA-seq datasets representing three tumor types were retrieved and processed uniformly. A meta-analysis was performed to assess transcriptomic similarity among the tumor cohorts. Differential gene expression analysis was carried out to characterize HPV-positive and HPV-negative expression patterns. Weighted gene co-expression network analysis (WGCNA) was performed to identify HPV-associated gene modules. Hub genes were selected using Module Membership and Gene Significance criteria and further prioritized through cohort-specific protein–protein interaction analysis. Results: Differential expression analysis identified distinct transcriptional differences associated with HPV status across all three cohorts, although the magnitude of differential expression varied between tumor types. Cross-cohort meta-analysis identified a 2959-gene transcriptional program reaching genome-wide significance under a fixed-effect model, with 557 genes remaining significant under a heterogeneity-adjusted random-effects model. Gene set enrichment analysis identified 1578 pathways reaching false discovery rate (FDR)-corrected significance, predominantly related to immune response, DNA repair, and DNA replication. WGCNA identified HPV-associated co-expression modules whose hub genes were enriched for immune regulation, adaptive immune activation, and antiviral host-defense processes. Conclusions: Integrated differential expression, heterogeneity-aware meta-analysis, and co-expression network analyses identified transcriptional patterns associated with HPV status across the included cohorts. These findings provide candidate genes and biological pathways for future validation and functional investigation in independent cohorts of HPV-associated cancers.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
HPV-positive tumors frequently exhibit distinct clinical characteristics.
Evidence base and method
A meta-analysis was performed to assess transcriptomic similarity among the tumor cohorts.
Consensus and conclusions
Differential expression analysis identified distinct transcriptional differences associated with HPV status across all three cohorts, although the magnitude of differential expression varied between tumor types.
Controversies and gaps
Integrated differential expression, heterogeneity-aware meta-analysis, and co-expression network analyses identified transcriptional patterns associated with HPV status across the included cohorts.
Research and practice implications
These findings provide candidate genes and biological pathways for future validation and functional investigation in independent cohorts of HPV-associated cancers.

高危型HPV 阳性女性 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化表现的荟萃分析

Meta-analysis of PAX1/JAM3 methylation performance in high-risk HPV-positive women
作者： Lianxing Qi, Yinfang Wu, Minxia Luo
摘要中文翻译背景:DNA 甲基化是 宫颈癌 筛查的新兴生物标志物。本研究旨在评估配对盒基因1/连接粘附分子3(JAM3)双基因甲基化分析对检测高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变和宫颈癌的诊断性能,并探讨其作为高危人乳头瘤病毒阳性女性分诊生物标志物的潜在用途。方法:在包括 PubMed 在内的 5 个数据库中进行了系统检索,以查找 宫颈癌 筛选中 PAX1/JAM3 甲基化检测的研究。通过 QUADAS-2 工具进行质量评估后,使用 Meta-Disc 1.4 和 Stata 17 软件进行统计分析。结果:荟萃分析中纳入了涉及 7494 名参与者的 8 项研究。对于诊断 2 级及以上宫颈上皮内瘤变,汇总敏感性为 0.81(95% CI:0.73-0.88),特异性为 0.95(95% CI:0.94-0.96),曲线下面积为 0.96。对于诊断 3 级及以上宫颈上皮内瘤变,汇总敏感性为 0.85(95% CI:0.75-0.92),特异性为 0.88(95% CI:0.86-0.89),曲线下面积为 0.90。结论:PAX1/JAM3 双基因甲基化检测对检测高级别宫颈上皮内瘤变和 宫颈癌 具有较高的诊断准确性,支持其作为高危 人乳头瘤病毒 阳性女性分诊生物标志物的潜在作用。

Meta-analysis of PAX1/JAM3 methylation performance in high-risk HPV-positive women

Authors: Lianxing Qi, Yinfang Wu, Minxia Luo
Original abstract (English)
Background: DNA methylation is an emerging biomarker for cervical cancer screening. This study aimed to evaluate the diagnostic performance of paired box gene 1/junctional adhesion molecule 3 (JAM3) dual-gene methylation analysis for detecting high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and cervical cancer, and to explore its potential utility as a triage biomarker for high-risk human papillomavirus positive women. Methods: A systematic search was conducted across 5 databases, including PubMed, for studies on PAX1/JAM3 methylation testing in cervical cancer screening. Following quality assessment via the QUADAS-2 tool, statistical analyses were performed using Meta-Disc 1.4 and Stata 17 software. Results: Eight studies involving 7494 participants were included in the meta-analysis. For diagnosing cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 and above the pooled sensitivity was 0.81 (95% CI: 0.73–0.88), specificity was 0.95 (95% CI: 0.94–0.96), and the area under the curve was 0.96. For diagnosing cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 3 and above the pooled sensitivity was 0.85 (95% CI: 0.75–0.92), specificity was 0.88 (95% CI: 0.86–0.89), and the area under the curve was 0.90. Conclusions: PAX1/JAM3 dual-gene methylation testing demonstrates high diagnostic accuracy for detecting high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and cervical cancer, supporting its potential role as a triage biomarker in high-risk human papillomavirus positive women.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method
A systematic search was conducted across 5 databases, including PubMed, for studies on PAX1/JAM3 methylation testing in cervical cancer screening.
Consensus and conclusions
For diagnosing cervical intraepithelial neoplasia grade 2 and above the pooled sensitivity was 0.81 (95% CI: 0.73–0.88), specificity was 0.95 (95% CI: 0.94–0.96), and the area under the curve was 0.96.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
PAX1/JAM3 dual-gene methylation testing demonstrates high diagnostic accuracy for detecting high-grade cervical intraepithelial neoplasia and cervical cancer, supporting its potential role as a triage biomarker in high-risk human papillomavirus positive women.

隐形负担:中国女性性别卫生劳动促进男性HPV 疫苗接种。

Invisible burden: Women's gendered health labor in promoting male HPV vaccination in China.
作者： Yi-Ning Zheng, Hao Gao
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 疫苗接种 扩展到男性代表了中国公共卫生领域的重大转变。然而,男性 HPV 疫苗接种 的宣传可能会让女性伴侣成为信息寻求者、传播者和促进者,从而引发有关健康相关责任的性别分配的问题。本研究利用 402 名中国女性的调查数据,探讨了媒体曝光和健康信念模型 (HBM) 结构与女性宣传男性 HPV 疫苗接种 的意愿之间的关系。结构方程模型显示,媒体接触与自我效能(β = 0.228,p < .001)和感知障碍(β = 0.295,p < .001)呈正相关。感知到的好处(β = 0.402,p < .001)和自我效能(β = 0.219,p < .001)是疫苗接种晋升意图的最强正向预测因子,而感知到的障碍与意图没有显着相关(β = -0.073,p = .087)。这些发现表明,女性伴侣主导的 疫苗接种 晋升可能是由认知评估和性别护理期望共同决定的。该研究通过将 HBM​​ 研究应用于亲密关系中面向他人的 疫苗接种 促进来扩展 HBM 研究,并提供与隐形健康劳动框架一致的间接证据。公共卫生传播和 疫苗接种 政策应直接面向男性,并减少对女性无偿健康促进工作的依赖。

提供的证据没有明确说明审查方法、数据库、资格流程或证据基础。

Invisible burden: Women's gendered health labor in promoting male HPV vaccination in China.

Authors: Yi-Ning Zheng, Hao Gao
Original abstract (English)
The expansion of human papillomavirus (HPV) vaccination to males represents an important shift in public health in China. However, promotion of male HPV vaccination may involve female partners as information seekers, communicators, and facilitators, raising questions about the gendered distribution of health-related responsibility. Drawing on survey data from 402 Chinese women, this study examines how media exposure and Health Belief Model (HBM) constructs are associated with women's intention to promote male HPV vaccination. Structural equation modeling showed that media exposure was positively associated with both self-efficacy (β = 0.228, p < .001) and perceived barriers (β = 0.295, p < .001). Perceived benefits (β = 0.402, p < .001) and self-efficacy (β = 0.219, p < .001) were the strongest positive predictors of vaccination promotion intention, whereas perceived barriers were not significantly associated with intention (β = -0.073, p = .087). These findings suggest that women's partner-directed vaccination promotion may be shaped by both cognitive evaluations and gendered caregiving expectations. The study extends HBM research by applying it to other-oriented vaccination promotion within intimate relationships and provides indirect evidence consistent with the invisible health labor framework. Public health communication and vaccination policy should address men directly and reduce reliance on women's unpaid health promotion work.
View review five-element analysis

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

C 补充信息学术文献 · 综述C supplementaryAcademic literature · Review

[局灶性上皮增生-赫克病]。

[Focal epithelial hyperplasia-Heck's disease].
作者： Steffi Silling, Ulrike Wieland, Alexander Kreuter
摘要中文翻译局灶性上皮增生 (FEH),也称为赫克氏病,是一种罕见的良性口腔粘膜疾病。其特征是多发、柔软、肤色或白色丘疹或扁平结节,主要出现在嘴唇、脸颊和舌头的粘膜上。这种情况的 患病率 在中美洲和南美洲的土著人群中特别高,导致除了家族性 传播 之外,还引发了一些关于遗传易感性的讨论。然而,目前世界各地报告的病例数量正在增加,特别是来自欧洲的病例。FEH 主要发生在儿童和青少年中,但老年人也受到影响。人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) HPV13 或 HPV32 型感染已被确定为 FEH 的病因。基于临床表现、组织学和 HPV 基因分型的赫克病的明确 诊断 以及排除其他口腔表现的差异 诊断 允许采用保守的 治疗 方法,因为致病 HPV 类型不具有致癌潜力,并且病变通常会自发消退。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
局灶性上皮增生 (FEH),也称为赫克氏病,是一种罕见的良性口腔粘膜疾病。其特征是多发、柔软、肤色或白色丘疹或扁平结节,主要出现在嘴唇、脸颊和舌头的粘膜上。这种情况的 患病率 在中美洲和南美洲的土著人群中特别高,导致除了家族性 传播 之外,还引发了一些关于遗传易感性的讨论。
证据基础与方法
该评论以摘要为基础,摘要提供了 FEH 的一般背景信息。证据包括对该病症的描述、其临床特征及其与 人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 型 HPV13 或 HPV32 的关联。该综述不报告具体数据库、检索日期、资格标准或荟萃分析方法。
共识与结论
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) HPV13 或 HPV32 型感染已被确定为 FEH 的病因。FEH 主要发生在儿童和青少年中,但老年人也受到影响。致病的 HPV 类型不具有致癌潜力,病变通常会自行消退。
争议与缺口
目前,世界各地报告的病例数量正在增加,尤其是欧洲。这表明流行病学模式可能发生转变,但审查没有提供足够的证据来确定这种增加的原因或其影响。
科研与实践启示
基于临床表现、组织学和 HPV 基因分型的赫克病的明确 诊断 以及排除其他口腔表现的差异 诊断 允许采用保守的 治疗 方法。

[Focal epithelial hyperplasia-Heck's disease].

Authors: Steffi Silling, Ulrike Wieland, Alexander Kreuter
Original abstract (English)
Focal epithelial hyperplasia (FEH), also known as Heck's disease, is a rare, benign condition of the oral mucosa. It is characterized by multiple, soft, skin-colored or whitish papules or flat nodules that occur primarily on the mucosa of the lips, cheeks, and tongue. The prevalence of this condition is particularly high among indigenous populations of Central and South America, leading to some discussion of a genetic predisposition in addition to familial transmission. However, the number of reported cases from all parts of the world is currently increasing, particularly from Europe. FEH occurs primarily in children and adolescents, but older adults are also affected. Infection with human papillomavirus (HPV) types HPV13 or HPV32 has been identified as the cause of FEH. A definitive diagnosis of Heck's disease based on clinical presentation, histology, and HPV genotyping, as well as the differential diagnosis to rule out other oral manifestations allow for a conservative treatment approach, as the causative HPV types do not have oncogenic potential and the lesions often resolve spontaneously.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Focal epithelial hyperplasia (FEH), also known as Heck's disease, is a rare, benign condition of the oral mucosa. It is characterized by multiple, soft, skin-colored or whitish papules or flat nodules that occur primarily on the mucosa of the lips, cheeks, and tongue. The prevalence of this condition is particularly high among indigenous populations of Central and South America, leading to some discussion of a genetic predisposition in addition to familial transmission.
Evidence base and method
The review is based on the abstract, which provides general background information on FEH. The evidence includes descriptions of the condition, its clinical features, and its association with human papillomavirus (HPV) types HPV13 or HPV32. The review does not report specific databases, search dates, eligibility criteria, or meta-analysis methods.
Consensus and conclusions
Infection with human papillomavirus (HPV) types HPV13 or HPV32 has been identified as the cause of FEH. FEH occurs primarily in children and adolescents, but older adults are also affected. The causative HPV types do not have oncogenic potential and the lesions often resolve spontaneously.
Controversies and gaps
The number of reported cases from all parts of the world is currently increasing, particularly from Europe. This suggests a potential shift in epidemiological patterns, but the review does not provide sufficient evidence to determine the reasons for this increase or its implications.
Research and practice implications
A definitive diagnosis of Heck's disease based on clinical presentation, histology, and HPV genotyping, as well as the differential diagnosis to rule out other oral manifestations allow for a conservative treatment approach.

妇科癌症中的免疫代谢相互作用:机制、生物标志物和治疗意义。

Immunometabolic interactions in gynecologic cancers: Mechanisms, biomarkers, and therapeutic implications.
作者： Agapiti H Chuwa, David H Mvunta
摘要中文翻译这篇叙述性综述综合了妇科癌症免疫代谢相互作用的最新证据,重点是它们对肿瘤生物学、治疗反应和全球癌症护理的影响。新数据表明,全身代谢功能障碍与肿瘤微环境免疫重塑密切相关。主要特征包括糖酵解增强、营养竞争、脂质积累和缺氧,这些特征共同损害细胞毒性 T 细胞功能,促进免疫耗竭,并支持免疫抑制性骨髓表型。尽管妇科恶性肿瘤具有共同的免疫代谢特征,但不同的肿瘤特异性模式是明显的。宫颈癌的特点是人乳头瘤病毒相关的代谢重编程和免疫检查点激活;卵巢癌表现出对脂质代谢和网膜脂肪相互作用的依赖性;子宫内膜癌与肥胖驱动的胰岛素抵抗和内分泌炎症信号密切相关。本综述评估了潜在的生物标志物和治疗策略,包括二甲双胍、他汀类药物和基于免疫检查点抑制剂的组合,并明确区分了临床前发现和临床建立的干预措施。目前代谢靶向的证据在很大程度上仍处于探索性,不同肿瘤类型的临床验证程度不同。此外,本次审查强调了低收入和中等收入国家的实施挑战,特别是获得先进分子诊断和免疫疗法的机会有限,并讨论了可扩展、低成本代谢方法的机会。最后,我们概述了未来的研究重点,包括空间多组学、纵向代谢分析和计算建模方法,以提高患者分层和预测准确性。总体而言,免疫代谢代表了一个有前途但不断发展的框架,可提高对妇科癌症的理解和管理,对精准肿瘤学和全球健康公平具有重要影响。

证据基础与方法
新数据表明,全身代谢功能障碍与肿瘤微环境免疫重塑密切相关。
共识与结论
主要特征包括糖酵解增强、营养竞争、脂质积累和缺氧,这些特征共同损害细胞毒性 T 细胞功能、促进免疫衰竭并支持免疫抑制性骨髓表型。尽管妇科恶性肿瘤具有共同的免疫代谢特征,但不同的肿瘤特异性模式是明显的。
争议与缺口
目前代谢靶向的证据在很大程度上仍处于探索性,不同肿瘤类型的临床验证程度不同。
科研与实践启示
本综述评估了潜在的生物标志物和治疗策略,包括二甲双胍、他汀类药物和基于免疫检查点抑制剂的组合,并明确区分了临床前发现和临床建立的干预措施。

Immunometabolic interactions in gynecologic cancers: Mechanisms, biomarkers, and therapeutic implications.

Authors: Agapiti H Chuwa, David H Mvunta
Original abstract (English)
This narrative review synthesizes current evidence on immunometabolic interactions in gynecologic cancers, with emphasis on their implications for tumor biology, therapeutic response, and global cancer care. Emerging data indicate that systemic metabolic dysfunction is closely integrated with tumor microenvironment immune remodeling. Key features include enhanced glycolysis, nutrient competition, lipid accumulation, and hypoxia, which collectively impair cytotoxic T-cell function, promote immune exhaustion, and support immunosuppressive myeloid phenotypes.Although gynecologic malignancies share common immunometabolic hallmarks, distinct tumor-specific patterns are evident. Cervical cancer is characterized by human papillomavirus-associated metabolic reprogramming and immune checkpoint activation; ovarian cancer demonstrates dependence on lipid metabolism and omental adipose interactions; and endometrial cancer is strongly associated with obesity-driven insulin resistance and endocrine-inflammatory signaling. This review evaluates potential biomarkers and therapeutic strategies, including metformin, statins, and immune checkpoint inhibitor-based combinations, and clearly distinguishes between preclinical findings and clinically established interventions. Current evidence for metabolic targeting remains largely exploratory, with variable degrees of clinical validation across tumor types. In addition, this review highlights implementation challenges in low- and middle-income countries, particularly limited access to advanced molecular diagnostics and immunotherapies, and discusses opportunities for scalable, low-cost metabolic approaches. Finally, we outline future research priorities, including spatial multiomics, longitudinal metabolic profiling, and computational modeling approaches to improve patient stratification and predictive accuracy. Overall, immunometabolism represents a promising but evolving framework for improving understanding and management of gynecologic cancers, with important implications for both precision oncology and global health equity.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This narrative review synthesizes current evidence on immunometabolic interactions in gynecologic cancers, with emphasis on their implications for tumor biology, therapeutic response, and global cancer care.
Evidence base and method
Emerging data indicate that systemic metabolic dysfunction is closely integrated with tumor microenvironment immune remodeling.
Consensus and conclusions
Key features include enhanced glycolysis, nutrient competition, lipid accumulation, and hypoxia, which collectively impair cytotoxic T-cell function, promote immune exhaustion, and support immunosuppressive myeloid phenotypes. Although gynecologic malignancies share common immunometabolic hallmarks, distinct tumor-specific patterns are evident.
Controversies and gaps
Current evidence for metabolic targeting remains largely exploratory, with variable degrees of clinical validation across tumor types.
Research and practice implications
This review evaluates potential biomarkers and therapeutic strategies, including metformin, statins, and immune checkpoint inhibitor-based combinations, and clearly distinguishes between preclinical findings and clinically established interventions.

HPV 相关恶性肿瘤免疫治疗的进展:新兴策略和临床进展

Advances in immunotherapy for HPV-associated malignancies: emerging strategies and clinical progress
作者： Alaa Alsalloum, Alena P. Zakhareva, Julia Lopatnikova, Sergey Sennikov
摘要中文翻译人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是大多数宫颈癌、口咽癌、肛门癌和外阴癌的主要病因。高危型HPV 类型的持续感染会促进免疫逃避、恶性转化和肿瘤进展。目前获得许可的预防性 HPV 疫苗 基于 L1 病毒样颗粒,通过诱导中和抗体,可以非常有效地预防新感染和 HPV 相关肿瘤疾病。然而,它们并不能根除已发生的感染或先前存在的病变。这些限制推动了治疗性 HPV 疫苗 和过继性 T 细胞策略的发展,旨在引发针对 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤的强大细胞介导免疫。病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 是有吸引力的治疗靶点。它们破坏细胞周期控制,并在 HPV 驱动的癌前病变和癌性病变中持续表达。在这篇综述中,我们全面概述了治疗性 HPV 疫苗,包括病毒和细菌 媒介、DNA 和 RNA 构建体、肽/蛋白质 疫苗 和基于细胞的方法,所有这些都旨在针对 E6 和 E7,同时考虑每个平台的优点和局限性。我们还对过继性 T 细胞疗法进行了深入分析,重点介绍了Immuno-STAT,这是一种肽-HLA 融合平台,用于直接扩增 HPV 特异性 CD8 + T 细胞。此外,我们讨论了将这些方式与免疫检查点抑制剂和其他免疫调节剂(包括双功能 PD-L1/TGF-β 抑制剂 bintrafusp alfa)相结合的合理组合策略,以克服关键障碍并提高临床疗效。总的来说,这篇综述综合了当前的证据,并概述了针对癌症 治疗 开发更精确定制的 HPV 特异性免疫疗法的未来方向。

范围与问题
人乳头瘤病毒 (HPV) 是大多数宫颈癌、口咽癌、肛门癌和外阴癌的主要病因。高危型HPV 类型的持续感染会促进免疫逃避、恶性转化和肿瘤进展。
证据基础与方法
本综述全面概述了治疗性 HPV 疫苗 和过继性 T 细胞策略,旨在引发针对 HPV 相关恶性肿瘤的强大细胞介导免疫。它还讨论了将这些方式与免疫检查点抑制剂和其他免疫调节剂相结合的合理组合策略。

目前获得许可的预防性 HPV 疫苗 通过诱导中和抗体,在预防新感染和 HPV 相关肿瘤性疾病方面非常有效。然而,它们并不能根除已发生的感染或先前存在的病变。这些限制推动了治疗性 HPV 疫苗 和过继性 T 细胞策略的发展。

病毒癌蛋白 E6 和 E7 是有吸引力的治疗靶点,因为它们破坏细胞周期控制,并且在 HPV 驱动的癌前病变和癌性病变中持续表达。然而,该领域存在局限性和证据差距,特别是在这些方式与免疫检查点抑制剂和其他免疫调节剂的整合方面。

这篇综述综合了当前的证据,并概述了针对癌症 治疗 开发更精确定制的 HPV 特异性免疫疗法的未来方向。它强调需要合理的组合策略,将治疗性 HPV 疫苗 和过继性 T 细胞疗法与免疫检查点抑制剂和其他免疫调节剂结合起来。

Advances in immunotherapy for HPV-associated malignancies: emerging strategies and clinical progress

Authors: Alaa Alsalloum, Alena P. Zakhareva, Julia Lopatnikova, Sergey Sennikov
Original abstract (English)
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the principal etiological factor in the majority of cervical, oropharyngeal, anal, and vulvar cancers. Persistent infection with high-risk HPV types promotes immune evasion, malignant transformation, and tumor progression. Currently licensed prophylactic HPV vaccines, based on L1 virus-like particles, are highly effective at preventing new infections and HPV-associated neoplastic disease through the induction of neutralizing antibodies. However, they do not eradicate established infections or preexisting lesions. These limitations have driven the development of therapeutic HPV vaccines and adoptive T-cell strategies aimed at eliciting robust cell-mediated immunity against HPV-associated malignancies. The viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 are attractive therapeutic targets. They disrupt cell-cycle control and are consistently expressed in HPV-driven precancerous and cancerous lesions. In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview of therapeutic HPV vaccines—including viral and bacterial vectors, DNA and RNA constructs, peptide/protein vaccines, and cell-based approaches—all designed to target E6 and E7, while considering the benefits and limitations of each platform. We also present an in-depth analysis of adoptive T-cell therapies, highlighting Immuno-STAT, a peptide–HLA fusion platform for the direct expansion of HPV-specific CD8 + T cells. Furthermore, we discuss rational combination strategies that integrate these modalities with immune checkpoint inhibitors and other immunomodulatory agents, including the bifunctional PD-L1/TGF-β inhibitor bintrafusp alfa, to overcome key barriers and enhance clinical efficacy. Overall, this review synthesizes current evidence and outlines future directions for the development of more precisely tailored HPV-specific immunotherapies in cancer treatment.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Human papillomavirus (HPV) is the principal etiological factor in the majority of cervical, oropharyngeal, anal, and vulvar cancers. Persistent infection with high-risk HPV types promotes immune evasion, malignant transformation, and tumor progression.
Evidence base and method
This review provides a comprehensive overview of therapeutic HPV vaccines and adoptive T-cell strategies aimed at eliciting robust cell-mediated immunity against HPV-associated malignancies. It also discusses rational combination strategies that integrate these modalities with immune checkpoint inhibitors and other immunomodulatory agents.
Consensus and conclusions
Currently licensed prophylactic HPV vaccines are highly effective at preventing new infections and HPV-associated neoplastic disease through the induction of neutralizing antibodies. However, they do not eradicate established infections or preexisting lesions. These limitations have driven the development of therapeutic HPV vaccines and adoptive T-cell strategies.
Controversies and gaps
The viral oncoproteins E6 and E7 are attractive therapeutic targets as they disrupt cell-cycle control and are consistently expressed in HPV-driven precancerous and cancerous lesions. However, there are limitations and evidence gaps in the field, particularly in the integration of these modalities with immune checkpoint inhibitors and other immunomodulatory agents.
Research and practice implications
This review synthesizes current evidence and outlines future directions for the development of more precisely tailored HPV-specific immunotherapies in cancer treatment. It highlights the need for rational combination strategies that integrate therapeutic HPV vaccines and adoptive T-cell therapies with immune checkpoint inhibitors and other immunomodulatory agents.
